# Server Specification

> Generated by openlore v1.2.0 on 2026-11-07 15:24
> Source files: source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py, source/server/exvius_field_treasures.py, source/server/player_state.py

## Purpose

Hosts the synthetic game server, handles client HTTP requests, and configures the data and state
used to serve responses.

## Entities

### VisionCard

> `source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

Represents an equipable card instance providing stat augmentations and passives, progressing through
rarity-based experience curves.

**Properties:**

| Name | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| id | string | Unique identifier of the vision card instance |
| card_id | string | Reference identifier to the master vision card definition |
| rarity | integer | Rarity tier determining experience progression requirements |
| exp | integer | Current experience points within the level |
| total_exp | integer | Cumulative lifetime experience points earned |
| level | integer | Current level of the vision card |
| is_equipped | boolean | Flag indicating whether the card is currently equipped on a unit |

**Relationships:**

- equipped-to Unit (Card is equipped onto an active unit in the roster)

### CraftSlot

> `source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

Represents an active crafting workstation slot used to synthesize items and equipment from recipes
over time.

**Properties:**

| Name | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| slot_id | integer | Crafting bench slot index |
| recipe_id | string | Identifier of the recipe assigned for synthesis |
| start_time | integer | Unix timestamp marking when synthesis began |
| is_active | boolean | Indicates whether the craft slot is currently occupied |

**Relationships:**

- produces InventoryItem (Completed crafting generates the target item and optional byproducts)

### ExchangeShop

> `source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

Represents an event or permanent store catalog offering units, items, and materials in exchange for
tokens or medals.

**Properties:**

| Name | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| shop_id | string | Unique identifier of the exchange schedule or storefront |
| token_id | string | Accepted currency, medal, or point token identifier |
| stock_limit | integer | Maximum purchase quantity allowed per player |

**Relationships:**

- supplies InventoryItem (Exchange store awards items or equipment upon trading tokens)
- supplies Unit (Exchange store can grant units or trust moogles upon trading tokens)

### InventoryItem

> `source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

Represents an item, piece of equipment, or materia held in player inventory, tracking quantity and
equipment assignment.

**Properties:**

| Name | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| id | string | Identifier of the item or equipment definition |
| count | integer | Total quantity held in inventory |
| is_equipped | boolean | Indicates whether this item or materia is currently equipped on a unit |

**Relationships:**

- worn-by Unit (Item or materia can be equipped by an active unit)

### TreasureReward

> `source/server/exvius_field_treasures.py`

Represents a discrete reward item, currency, recipe, or equipment grant encoded within a treasure
chest reward definition.

**Properties:**

| Name | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| kind | integer | Category or channel type of the reward (e.g., standard item, currency, recipe). |
| id | integer | Specific item, equipment, recipe, or currency identifier. |
| count | integer | Quantity of the specified reward to grant. |

**Relationships:**

- belongs to FieldTreasure (Reward belongs to a parent field treasure definition.)

### PlayerState

> `source/server/player_state.py`

Represents the core player account and persistent game state, managing rank progression, resource
caps, orb regeneration pools, and owned entity collections.

**Properties:**

| Name | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| mode | string | Operational persistence mode ('live', 'ephemeral', or 'frozen') |
| team_level | integer | Player rank/level determining progression and caps |
| team_exp | integer | Accumulated account experience points towards next rank |
| nrg_cap | integer | Derived stamina (NRG) capacity threshold based on rank |
| colosseum_orbs | integer | Current colosseum/arena action orbs |
| event_orbs | integer | Current event participation orbs |

**Relationships:**

- has-many Unit (Player manages an inventory and roster of units)
- has-many Beast (Player possesses awakened espers and beasts)
- has-many PartyDeck (Player configures battle decks and party presets)
- has-many Item (Player stores equipment, consumables, and summon tickets)

### Beast

> `source/server/player_state.py`

Represents a summonable Esper/Beast entity with leveling, training capacity points (CP), and an
unlockable skill board grid.

**Properties:**

| Name | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| beast_id | string | Unique identifier referencing the beast definition |
| level | integer | Current level of the beast |
| exp | integer | Accumulated beast experience points |
| cp | integer | Available capacity points used for learning grid skills |
| board_bits | string | Bitmask representing learned grid nodes and board pieces |

**Relationships:**

- belongs to PlayerState (Beast is unlocked and owned by the player)
- linked-to PartyDeck (Beast is bound to a deck to provide stats and summon abilities in combat)

### PartyDeck

> `source/server/player_state.py`

Represents a battle party configuration composed of ordered unit positions and an assigned
Esper/Beast.

**Properties:**

| Name | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| deck_id | string | Unique identifier for the party preset or deck |
| order | integer | Display and evaluation index of the party preset |
| members | array | Ordered references to unit instance IDs assigned to party slots |
| beast_id | string | ID of the Esper/Beast linked to this deck |

**Relationships:**

- belongs to PlayerState (Party deck belongs to player profile configurations)
- contains Unit (Deck assigns roster units to combat positions)
- links-to Beast (Deck links a selected beast for battle)

### Item

> `source/server/player_state.py`

Represents an inventory asset, including consumable supplies, equipment, materia, awakening
materials, and gacha summon tickets.

**Properties:**

| Name | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| item_id | string | Identifier referencing the item definition in master data |
| count | integer | Quantity currently owned in player inventory |
| category | string | Classification such as important item, equipment, materia, or ticket |

**Relationships:**

- belongs to PlayerState (Items reside in player inventory collections)
- used-by GachaBanner (Summon tickets are spent on gacha banners to summon units)

## Requirements

### Requirement: VisionCardValidation

The system SHALL validate VisionCard according to these rules:
- Equipped vision cards cannot be consumed as enhancement fodder during mixing
- Experience progression per level conforms to CARD_RARITY_EXP rate thresholds
- Master card removal detaches card from unit and returns it to player inventory via mail

#### Scenario: MixVisionCards
- **GIVEN** Player owns a base vision card and unequipped fodder cards
- **WHEN** vision_card_mix is performed
- **THEN** Fodder cards are consumed and base card gains level and experience

#### Scenario: ReleaseMasterCard
- **GIVEN** A unit with an equipped master card
- **WHEN** master_card_release is requested
- **THEN** Master card is detached and returned as an attachment in player mail

### Requirement: CraftSlotValidation

The system SHALL validate CraftSlot according to these rules:
- Initiating crafting requires consuming required recipe ingredients and currency upfront
- Cancelling an active craft does not refund consumed ingredients or fees when AUTHORED_CRAFT_CANCEL_REFUND is false
- Craft synthesis completion requires elapsed duration or spending charge tokens to fast-complete

#### Scenario: StartCraft
- **GIVEN** An idle crafting slot and sufficient recipe ingredients in inventory
- **WHEN** craft_start is called
- **THEN** Slot is marked active with start time and materials are deducted

#### Scenario: CancelCraft
- **GIVEN** A craft slot currently synthesizing an item
- **WHEN** craft_cancel is called
- **THEN** Slot is cleared to idle state without refunding crafting ingredients

#### Scenario: CompleteCraft
- **GIVEN** A crafting slot whose required synthesis duration has elapsed
- **WHEN** craft_end is called
- **THEN** Target crafted item and byproducts are granted to inventory and slot is freed

### Requirement: ExchangeShopValidation

The system SHALL validate ExchangeShop according to these rules:
- Purchasing requires having sufficient medal or token currency balance
- Player cannot exceed designated item purchase stock limits
- Unit exchanges must verify the traded unit is not deployed

#### Scenario: ExchangeTokensForItems
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Player holds sufficient point tokens and listing has remaining stock
- **WHEN** shop_exchange_item or medal_exchange is called
- **THEN** Tokens are deducted and requested items are added to player inventory

#### Scenario: ExchangeUnitForMaterials
- **GIVEN** Player owns an eligible non-deployed unit
- **WHEN** shop_exchange_unit is called
- **THEN** Unit is removed from player roster and reward items are granted

### Requirement: InventoryItemValidation

The system SHALL validate InventoryItem according to these rules:
- Equipped weapons, armor, and materia cannot be sold until unequipped
- Item sale settlements must execute atomically against projected inventory counts

#### Scenario: SellInventoryItems
- **GIVEN** Player has unequipped items in inventory
- **WHEN** item_sell is executed
- **THEN** Item count is decremented and gil currency is added to player balance

#### Scenario: BuyShopItems
- **GIVEN** Player has sufficient gil currency and shop has available inventory
- **WHEN** item_buy is executed
- **THEN** Gil currency is deducted and purchased item count increases in inventory

### Requirement: TreasureRewardValidation

The system SHALL validate TreasureReward according to these rules:
- Must be deserialized from a valid colon-delimited 'kind:id:count' triple.
- Reward kind must be supported by recognized chest distribution channels or player currency channels.

#### Scenario: ParseRewardTripleFromTreasureDefinition
- **GIVEN** A field treasure containing encoded reward string '20:1001:5'
- **WHEN** Rewards are deserialized for crediting
- **THEN** A TreasureReward instance with kind 20, id 1001, and count 5 is instantiated

### Requirement: PlayerStateValidation

The system SHALL validate PlayerState according to these rules:
- Mode must be one of 'live', 'ephemeral', or 'frozen'
- State mutations must be rejected when mode is 'frozen'
- Natural orb and NRG recovery must respect authored maximum capacity thresholds
- All units and beasts assigned to active decks must exist in player state collections

#### Scenario: LoadAndNormalizePlayerState
- **GIVEN** A saved player state data payload
- **WHEN** load() is invoked
- **THEN** State is upgraded to current schema version, normalized, and verified for state coherence

#### Scenario: ServeClientResponseProjection
- **GIVEN** An active player state
- **WHEN** apply_to_reply() is called
- **THEN** Projections for unit rows, deck rows, equipment, and orb statuses are assembled into the reply

### Requirement: BeastValidation

The system SHALL validate Beast according to these rules:
- Learning a board piece requires sufficient available beast CP
- Unlocking locked board pieces requires spending required unlock items
- Beast level cannot exceed the maximum level for its awakening rank

#### Scenario: LearnBeastSkillBoardPiece
- **GIVEN** An unlocked beast with available capacity points
- **WHEN** learn_beast_pieces() is called for designated piece IDs
- **THEN** The bitmask is updated with learned bits and corresponding CP is deducted

#### Scenario: EnhanceBeastWithMagiciteMaterials
- **GIVEN** An acquired beast and valid enhancement items
- **WHEN** mix_beast() is executed
- **THEN** Items are deducted, experience is credited, and CP is awarded upon leveling up

### Requirement: PartyDeckValidation

The system SHALL validate PartyDeck according to these rules:
- All assigned member instance IDs must correspond to valid units in player state
- Deck slot order and deck IDs within party settings must be unique
- Linked beast must be present in player's unlocked beasts

#### Scenario: LinkBeastToDeck
- **GIVEN** An existing party deck and an awakened beast in player state
- **WHEN** link_beast() is called with the beast ID
- **THEN** The deck's beast assignment is updated and saved

### Requirement: ItemValidation

The system SHALL validate Item according to these rules:
- Item count cannot be negative
- Spending items requires available inventory count greater than or equal to requested amount

#### Scenario: GrantItemsToInventory
- **GIVEN** A valid item identifier and quantity
- **WHEN** grant_item() is invoked
- **THEN** Player item count increases by the specified quantity

#### Scenario: SpendItemsFromInventory
- **GIVEN** Sufficient item quantity in player inventory
- **WHEN** spend_item() is invoked
- **THEN** Item quantity is decremented by the spent amount

> `source/server/battle_content.py`

### Requirement: IdentifyMenuResourceMission

The system SHALL the service shall identify banner-only mission records as ui resources rather than battles.

- **Implementation**: `is_menu_resource_row::source/server/battle_content.py`

#### Scenario: BannerOnlyMission
- **GIVEN** A mission record contains menu resource data without battle content
- **WHEN** the record is classified
- **THEN** it is identified as a UI resource

### Requirement: BuildStoryMissionBundle

The system SHALL the service shall create the battle-bundle reply for an original zero-wave story-only mission.

- **Implementation**: `story_mission_bundle::source/server/battle_content.py`

#### Scenario: ZeroWaveStoryMission
- **GIVEN** The mission is story-only and has no encounter waves
- **WHEN** its bundle is built
- **THEN** the reply preserves the carrier and represents the story mission without combat waves

### Requirement: SelectStoryMissionPhases

The system SHALL the service shall retain original non-combat mission phases that lead into story scenes.

- **Implementation**: `story_mission_phases::source/server/battle_content.py`

#### Scenario: StoryScenePhases
- **GIVEN** A mission has original non-combat phases leading to scenes
- **WHEN** story phases are selected
- **THEN** those phases are retained

### Requirement: SelectStoryMissionResources

The system SHALL the service shall select each story part's map and scene according to the mission's native progress field.

- **Implementation**: `story_mission_resources::source/server/battle_content.py`

#### Scenario: ProgressSelectsStoryPart
- **GIVEN** A mission has multiple story parts and a native progress value
- **WHEN** resources are selected
- **THEN** the map and scene for the applicable part are returned

### Requirement: BuildStoryEventBundle

The system SHALL the service shall create the battle-bundle reply for a story map event using its carrier without an encounter.

- **Implementation**: `story_event_bundle::source/server/battle_content.py`

#### Scenario: StoryEventWithoutEncounter
- **GIVEN** A story map event has a carrier and no encounter
- **WHEN** its bundle is built
- **THEN** the reply contains the carrier and no encounter


## Sub-components

> `BattleContentService` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: SystemExit

> Implements: `SystemExit`

Terminates mission derivation when a required fatal condition prevents producing a valid battle bundle.

#### Requirement: AbortDerivation

The system SHALL the service must stop derivation when it raises a fatal exit condition.

#### Scenario: FatalCondition
- **GIVEN** Mission derivation encounters a condition that requires termination
- **WHEN** SystemExit is raised
- **THEN** Derivation stops without returning a bundle

### Sub-component: UnescapeCell

> Implements: `unescape_cell`

Decodes a JSON-escaped cell value into its represented value.

#### Requirement: DecodeEscapedCell

The system SHALL the cell decoder must parse a json-encoded cell value.

#### Scenario: ValidEscapedString
- **GIVEN** A cell contains a valid JSON-escaped string
- **WHEN** The cell is decoded
- **THEN** The decoded value contains the represented string

### Sub-component: ReadLine

> Implements: `fh.readline`

Reads the next line from the current input stream.

#### Requirement: ReadNextLine

The system SHALL the input reader must return the next available line from the stream.

#### Scenario: LineAvailable
- **GIVEN** The stream has another line
- **WHEN** A line is read
- **THEN** The next line is returned

### Sub-component: FirstStartsWith

> Implements: `first.startswith`

Determines whether the first input value begins with a specified prefix.

#### Requirement: CheckPrefix

The system SHALL the prefix check must report whether the first value begins with the requested prefix.

#### Scenario: MatchingPrefix
- **GIVEN** The first value begins with the requested prefix
- **WHEN** The prefix check runs
- **THEN** The result is true

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses a JSON-formatted string into its corresponding data value.

#### Requirement: ParseJsonText

The system SHALL the parser must convert valid json text into its corresponding value.

#### Scenario: ValidJson
- **GIVEN** The input contains valid JSON text
- **WHEN** The text is parsed
- **THEN** The corresponding structured value is returned

### Sub-component: MetaGet

> Implements: `meta.get`

Retrieves a metadata value by key while allowing a default when the key is absent.

#### Requirement: RetrieveMetadata

The system SHALL metadata lookup must return the value associated with a key or its supplied default.

#### Scenario: MissingMetadataKey
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The metadata mapping does not contain the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved with a default
- **THEN** The default is returned

### Sub-component: IndexFieldsGet

> Implements: `INDEX_FIELDS.get`

Looks up the configured field mapping for an index field key.

#### Requirement: LookupIndexField

The system SHALL the index field lookup must return the configured mapping or the supplied default.

#### Scenario: ConfiguredField
- **GIVEN** The key exists in INDEX_FIELDS
- **WHEN** The mapping is looked up
- **THEN** Its configured field mapping is returned

### Sub-component: RstripLine

> Implements: `line.rstrip`

Removes trailing whitespace from an input line.

#### Requirement: TrimLineEnd

The system SHALL line normalization must remove trailing whitespace from the line.

#### Scenario: TrailingNewline
- **GIVEN** A line ends in a newline character
- **WHEN** Trailing whitespace is removed
- **THEN** The returned line has no trailing newline

### Sub-component: SplitLine

> Implements: `line.split`

Separates a line into fields using the requested delimiter.

#### Requirement: SeparateLineFields

The system SHALL the line parser must return the fields separated by the requested delimiter.

#### Scenario: MultipleFields
- **GIVEN** A line contains fields separated by the delimiter
- **WHEN** The line is split
- **THEN** Each field is returned in order

### Sub-component: AppendRow

> Implements: `rows.append`

Adds a parsed row to the accumulated row collection.

#### Requirement: AccumulateRow

The system SHALL the row accumulator must add the supplied row to the collection.

#### Scenario: AppendParsedRow
- **GIVEN** A row has been parsed
- **WHEN** The row is appended
- **THEN** The collection contains the row at its end

### Sub-component: AsSource

> Implements: `as_source`

Normalizes a source argument into an NDJSON source representation.

#### Requirement: NormalizeSource

The system SHALL source normalization must provide an ndjson source for supported source inputs and must reject incompatible input types.

#### Scenario: UnsupportedSourceType
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The source argument has an unsupported type
- **WHEN** It is normalized
- **THEN** A TypeError is raised

### Sub-component: LoadBreakStatus

> Implements: `load_break_status`

Loads break-status data and retrieves its document-level status information.

#### Requirement: ReadBreakStatus

The system SHALL the status loader must return break-status information from the loaded document.

#### Scenario: StatusDocumentLoaded
- **GIVEN** The source contains a valid break-status document
- **WHEN** Break status is loaded
- **THEN** The document's status information is returned

### Sub-component: ResolvePool

> Implements: `resolve_pool`

Selects the applicable pool candidates for battle content resolution.

#### Requirement: SelectPoolCandidates

The system SHALL pool resolution must select candidates that satisfy the requested battle criteria and return them in the required order.

#### Scenario: MatchingCandidates
- **GIVEN** Pool records include candidates matching the battle criteria
- **WHEN** The pool is resolved
- **THEN** Matching candidates are returned in the required order

### Sub-component: LoadTable

> Implements: `load`

Loads and combines table data with applicable event support, replacements, overrides, and base content.

#### Requirement: LoadBattleTable

The system SHALL table loading must return rows from the requested table with applicable event and override data incorporated.

#### Scenario: TableWithOverrides
- **GIVEN** A table has base rows and matching override data
- **WHEN** The table is loaded
- **THEN** The returned rows reflect the applicable override data

### Sub-component: PartsAllSetdefault

> Implements: `parts_all.setdefault`

Ensures the aggregate parts mapping has an entry for a requested key.

#### Requirement: EnsurePartsEntry

The system SHALL the parts mapping must preserve an existing entry or create one using the supplied default.

#### Scenario: MissingPartsEntry
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The key is absent from the parts mapping
- **WHEN** The entry is ensured
- **THEN** A new entry is created with the supplied default

### Sub-component: PartsAllValues

> Implements: `parts_all.values`

Provides the values held in the aggregate parts mapping.

#### Requirement: EnumerateParts

The system SHALL the parts mapping must expose all stored part collections as values.

#### Scenario: MappingHasParts
- **GIVEN** The mapping contains part collections
- **WHEN** Its values are retrieved
- **THEN** All stored part collections are available

### Sub-component: SortRows

> Implements: `rows.sort`

Orders rows according to the configured row ordering.

#### Requirement: OrderRows

The system SHALL row ordering must arrange rows according to the applicable sort key.

#### Scenario: OutOfOrderRows
- **GIVEN** Rows are not in the required order
- **WHEN** The rows are sorted
- **THEN** They are ordered according to the applicable sort key

### Sub-component: RecordGet

> Implements: `r.get`

Retrieves a value from a record by key with an optional default.

#### Requirement: RetrieveRecordValue

The system SHALL record lookup must return the keyed value or the optional default.

#### Scenario: RecordKeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The record contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: PartGet

> Implements: `p.get`

Retrieves a value from a part record by key with an optional default.

#### Requirement: RetrievePartValue

The system SHALL part lookup must return the keyed value or the optional default.

#### Scenario: PartKeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The part record contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: RowSetdefault

> Implements: `row.setdefault`

Ensures a row has a value for a specified key.

#### Requirement: EnsureRowField

The system SHALL row normalization must preserve an existing field or populate it with the supplied default.

#### Scenario: MissingRowField
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A row does not contain the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is ensured
- **THEN** The default value is assigned

### Sub-component: AppendPhaseRow

> Implements: `phase_rows.append`

Adds a phase row to the accumulated phase rows.

#### Requirement: AccumulatePhase

The system SHALL the phase row accumulator must add each accepted phase row to its collection.

#### Scenario: AcceptedPhase
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A phase row has been accepted for the battle
- **WHEN** It is appended
- **THEN** The phase rows contain it

### Sub-component: GroupGet

> Implements: `g.get`

Retrieves a value from a group record by key with an optional default.

#### Requirement: RetrieveGroupValue

The system SHALL group lookup must return the keyed value or the optional default.

#### Scenario: GroupKeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The group record contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: GroupIdsSetdefault

> Implements: `group_ids.setdefault`

Ensures the group identifier mapping contains an entry for the requested key.

#### Requirement: EnsureGroupIdEntry

The system SHALL the group identifier mapping must preserve an existing entry or create one using the supplied default.

#### Scenario: MissingGroupEntry
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested key is absent
- **WHEN** A group identifier entry is ensured
- **THEN** The default entry is created

### Sub-component: AddPartId

> Implements: `part_ids.add`

Adds a part identifier to the set of identifiers associated with the battle.

#### Requirement: RecordPartId

The system SHALL the identifier collection must contain the supplied part identifier after it is added.

#### Scenario: NewPartId
- **GIVEN** A part identifier is not yet in the set
- **WHEN** It is added
- **THEN** The set contains the identifier

### Sub-component: PartsAllGet

> Implements: `parts_all.get`

Retrieves the aggregate part collection for a key with an optional default.

#### Requirement: LookupPartsCollection

The system SHALL the aggregate parts lookup must return the collection for the key or its default.

#### Scenario: PartsCollectionExists
- **GIVEN** The aggregate mapping contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The collection is retrieved
- **THEN** The corresponding part collection is returned

### Sub-component: PartRowGet

> Implements: `pr.get`

Retrieves a value from a part row by key with an optional default.

#### Requirement: RetrievePartRowValue

The system SHALL part-row lookup must return the keyed value or its optional default.

#### Scenario: PartRowKeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The part row contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: AppendDrop

> Implements: `drops.append`

Adds a resolved drop to the drop collection.

#### Requirement: AccumulateDrop

The system SHALL the drop accumulator must include each resolved drop supplied to it.

#### Scenario: ResolvedDrop
- **GIVEN** A drop has been resolved for the battle
- **WHEN** It is appended
- **THEN** The drops collection contains it

### Sub-component: AppendDropRow

> Implements: `drop_rows.append`

Adds a drop row to the accumulated drop rows.

#### Requirement: AccumulateDropRow

The system SHALL the drop-row accumulator must include each accepted drop row supplied to it.

#### Scenario: AcceptedDropRow
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A drop row is accepted for the battle
- **WHEN** It is appended
- **THEN** The drop rows contain it

### Sub-component: RowGet

> Implements: `row.get`

Retrieves a value from a row by key with an optional default.

#### Requirement: RetrieveRowValue

The system SHALL row lookup must return the keyed value or its optional default.

#### Scenario: RowKeyMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The row does not contain the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved with a default

### Sub-component: UpdatePartIds

> Implements: `part_ids.update`

Adds multiple part identifiers to the identifier set.

#### Requirement: RecordPartIds

The system SHALL the identifier collection must include every identifier in the supplied collection.

#### Scenario: MultiplePartIds
- **GIVEN** A collection contains multiple part identifiers
- **WHEN** The set is updated
- **THEN** All supplied identifiers are present

### Sub-component: GroupIdsValues

> Implements: `group_ids.values`

Provides the values held in the group identifier mapping.

#### Requirement: EnumerateGroupIds

The system SHALL the group identifier mapping must expose all stored values.

#### Scenario: MappingHasGroupIds
- **GIVEN** The mapping contains group identifier entries
- **WHEN** Its values are retrieved
- **THEN** All stored values are available

### Sub-component: MonsterByPartGet

> Implements: `mon_by_part.get`

Retrieves the monster record associated with a part key.

#### Requirement: LookupMonsterByPart

The system SHALL monster lookup must return the associated record or the optional default.

#### Scenario: MonsterMapped
- **GIVEN** A monster is mapped to the requested part key
- **WHEN** The monster is retrieved
- **THEN** The mapped monster record is returned

### Sub-component: AppendMonsterRow

> Implements: `monster_rows.append`

Adds a monster row to the accumulated monster rows.

#### Requirement: AccumulateMonsterRow

The system SHALL the monster-row accumulator must include each selected monster row supplied to it.

#### Scenario: SelectedMonsterRow
- **GIVEN** A monster row is selected for the battle
- **WHEN** It is appended
- **THEN** The monster rows contain it

### Sub-component: MonsterGet

> Implements: `m.get`

Retrieves a value from a monster record by key with an optional default.

#### Requirement: RetrieveMonsterValue

The system SHALL monster lookup must return the keyed value or its optional default.

#### Scenario: MonsterKeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The monster record contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved

> `source/server/capture_server.py`

### Requirement: HandlePostRequest

The system SHALL the server shall accept a client post request, interpret its endpoint and request body, and return the corresponding server response.

#### Scenario: POSTRequestReceived
- **GIVEN** The server is running and receives a POST request
- **WHEN** The request is handled
- **THEN** The server returns an HTTP response for the requested endpoint

### Requirement: HandleGetRequest

The system SHALL the server shall handle client get requests and return an http response appropriate to the requested path.

#### Scenario: GETRequestReceived
- **GIVEN** The server is running and receives a GET request
- **WHEN** The request is handled
- **THEN** The server returns an HTTP response

### Requirement: HandleHeadRequest

The system SHALL the server shall handle client head requests and return response headers without a response body.

#### Scenario: HEADRequestReceived
- **GIVEN** The server is running and receives a HEAD request
- **WHEN** The request is handled
- **THEN** The response contains headers and no body

### Requirement: StartServer

The system SHALL the server shall resolve startup options and data configuration, initialize the selected operating mode, and serve requests using the configured http or tls listener.

#### Scenario: StartWithValidConfiguration
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Valid startup arguments and required data are available
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** The server initializes the configured mode and serves requests

#### Scenario: PrintResolvedPaths
- **GIVEN** The print-paths option is supplied
- **WHEN** main is called
- **THEN** Resolved data paths are printed and the server does not bind a port

### Requirement: PrescanDataArgument

The system SHALL the server shall identify a data-root value supplied as either a separate command-line argument or an equals-form option.

#### Scenario: SeparateDataOption
- **GIVEN** Arguments contain --data followed by a path
- **WHEN** The arguments are prescanned
- **THEN** The path is returned

#### Scenario: EqualsFormDataOption
- **GIVEN** Arguments contain --data= followed by a path
- **WHEN** The arguments are prescanned
- **THEN** The path is returned

#### Scenario: DataOptionAbsent
- **GIVEN** Arguments contain no data option
- **WHEN** The arguments are prescanned
- **THEN** Null is returned


## Sub-components

> `CaptureServer` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Note

> Implements: `_note`

Records an explanatory note associated with the orchestrator.

#### Requirement: RecordNote

The system SHALL the block shall make its explanatory note available to the orchestrator.

#### Scenario: NoteIsRecorded
- **GIVEN** The orchestrator reaches the note block
- **WHEN** The block is evaluated
- **THEN** The explanatory note is available

### Sub-component: EndpointLoading

> Implements: `load_endpoints`

Loads endpoint configuration and builds lookup data by class and token.

#### Requirement: LoadEndpointMappings

The system SHALL the block shall load endpoint data from the supplied path and return class and token lookup mappings.

#### Scenario: ValidEndpointFile
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The supplied path identifies valid endpoint JSON
- **WHEN** Endpoint mappings are loaded
- **THEN** The result contains class and token mappings derived from the file

### Sub-component: ProcessExit

> Implements: `SystemExit`

Terminates execution when the orchestrator requests a process exit.

#### Requirement: TerminateProcess

The system SHALL the block must signal process termination with the requested exit status.

#### Scenario: ExitIsRequested
- **GIVEN** The orchestrator requests termination
- **WHEN** SystemExit is raised
- **THEN** Execution terminates with the requested status

### Sub-component: StateLoading

> Implements: `load_state`

Loads or initializes the single player state, applying migrations and configured grants before saving it.

#### Requirement: LoadOrInitializePlayerState

The system SHALL the block shall load player state from the configured state file, migrate supported legacy state, and use fresh state when loading or migration cannot be completed.

#### Scenario: LegacyStateMigration
<!-- openlore-test: priority=low (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The state file contains a supported version-one state
- **WHEN** The state is loaded
- **THEN** The state is migrated and made available as the player state

#### Scenario: InvalidStateFallback
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The state file is missing or invalid
- **WHEN** The state is loaded
- **THEN** A fresh player state is used

#### Requirement: ApplyConfiguredStateGrants

The system SHALL the block shall apply configured player, ticket, switch, beast, emblem, and item grants to the player state.

#### Scenario: ConfiguredGrantsAreApplied
- **GIVEN** State configuration contains valid grants
- **WHEN** The state setup completes
- **THEN** The player state includes those grants

#### Requirement: PersistPlayerState

The system SHALL the block shall save the resulting player state after setup.

#### Scenario: StateSetupCompletes
- **GIVEN** The player state has been loaded or initialized
- **WHEN** State setup completes
- **THEN** The resulting player state is saved

### Sub-component: TestRosterSeeding

> Implements: `_seed_max_test_roster`

Seeds the configured maximum test roster.

#### Requirement: SeedMaximumTestRoster

The system SHALL the block shall populate the test roster up to its configured maximum.

#### Scenario: RosterSeedingRequested
- **GIVEN** Test roster seeding is requested
- **WHEN** The block runs
- **THEN** The roster is populated up to the configured maximum

- **THEN** Seeding is reported as allowed

### Sub-component: JsonLoading

> `source/server/data_paths.py`

### Requirement: ResolveDataPaths

The system SHALL the server must resolve configured data paths using explicit environment overrides first, then paths relative to ffbe_data, then legacy checkout defaults. empty environment values must be treated as unset.

#### Scenario: RootRelativeDefaults
- **GIVEN** FFBE_DATA is set and a path-specific override is unset
- **WHEN** a data path is resolved
- **THEN** the path MUST point beneath the configured data root

#### Scenario: ExplicitOverridePrecedence
<!-- openlore-test: priority=low (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** a path-specific environment override is non-empty
- **WHEN** the path is resolved
- **THEN** the override MUST take precedence over root-relative and legacy paths

#### Scenario: LegacyDefaults
<!-- openlore-test: priority=low (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** FFBE_DATA and the path-specific override are unset
- **WHEN** the path is resolved
- **THEN** the path MUST use the legacy checkout location

### Requirement: ResolveExistingOverridePaths

The system SHALL the server must preserve the established semantics of existing path overrides by using their non-empty values verbatim, falling back to the data root or legacy default when unset.

#### Scenario: ExistingOverrideIsPreserved
- **GIVEN** An existing path override is non-empty
- **WHEN** the path is resolved
- **THEN** its value MUST be returned verbatim

#### Scenario: ExistingOverrideIsUnset
- **GIVEN** The override is unset and FFBE_DATA is set
- **WHEN** the path is resolved
- **THEN** the path MUST be rooted beneath FFBE_DATA

> `source/server/event_archive.py`

### Requirement: ListEvents

The system SHALL the service shall provide the event archive document and its event records, area identifiers, and published campaign listings.

- **Implementation**: `listings::source/server/event_archive.py`

#### Scenario: PublishedCampaignsRetainTheirAreas
- **GIVEN** The archive contains events belonging to published campaigns
- **WHEN** Campaign listings are requested
- **THEN** The service returns one tile per published campaign and retains every original area

### Requirement: BuildEventMenu

The system SHALL the service shall group archived events into selectable menu entries and show event-related area and reward information.

#### Scenario: MenuGroupsArchiveEntries
- **GIVEN** The archive contains events across its available groups
- **WHEN** The menu is requested for a player
- **THEN** The service returns selectable entries organized by group

#### Scenario: SelectedGroupFiltersMenuEntries
- **GIVEN** A valid archive group selection
- **WHEN** The menu is requested with that selection
- **THEN** The service returns entries for the selected group

### Requirement: ActivateArchivedMode

The system SHALL the service shall select a preserved season and prepare its original native mode configuration, including applicable player party data.

- **Implementation**: `activate_mode::source/server/event_archive.py`

#### Scenario: ActivateAValidArchivedSeason
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player selects a valid preserved season
- **WHEN** The archived mode is activated
- **THEN** The service prepares the original native mode configuration for that season

#### Scenario: RejectAnInvalidSeasonSelection
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player selects a season that is not available
- **WHEN** The archived mode is activated
- **THEN** The service raises a value error

#### Scenario: PreservePlayerPartyConfiguration
- **GIVEN** The selected mode supports special party groups
- **WHEN** The archived mode is activated
- **THEN** The player's applicable party configuration is included in the mode setup


## Sub-components

> `EventArchive` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: ModeActivation

> Implements: `activate_mode`

Selects a preserved season before opening its original native mode scene.

#### Requirement: SelectArchivedSeason

The system SHALL the system shall resolve the requested archive selection to its preserved season and prepare the corresponding native mode state.

#### Scenario: ValidArchiveSelection
- **GIVEN** The player selects a published preserved season with a matching native mode.
- **WHEN** The system activates the selection.
- **THEN** The season's original native mode state is prepared for the player.

#### Scenario: InvalidArchiveSelection
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The selection does not identify a valid preserved season.
- **WHEN** The system activates the selection.
- **THEN** The system reports the invalid selection with a ValueError.

#### Requirement: PreserveSeasonParty

The system SHALL the system shall retain the selected season's permitted special party groups and party data for the player.

#### Scenario: SeasonPartyIsAvailable
- **GIVEN** The selected season defines permitted special party groups and party data.
- **WHEN** The season is activated.
- **THEN** The player's state contains the selected season's allowed party groups and party.

### Sub-component: DefaultValueMap

> Implements: `defaultdict`

Provides a mapping that supplies a default value for an uninitialized key.

#### Requirement: ProvideDefaultForMissingKey

The system SHALL the mapping shall provide its configured default value when a key has no stored value.

#### Scenario: LookupMissingKey
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A key has not been stored in the mapping.
- **WHEN** The key is accessed.
- **THEN** The mapping provides the configured default value.

### Sub-component: ArchiveListings

> Implements: `listings`

Builds archive tiles for published campaigns while retaining each campaign's original areas.

#### Requirement: GroupPublishedCampaigns

The system SHALL the system shall produce one archive tile per published campaign and shall retain all areas belonging to that campaign.

#### Scenario: CampaignHasMultipleAreas
- **GIVEN** Published event rows include multiple areas for one campaign.
- **WHEN** The archive listings are built.
- **THEN** The campaign has one tile containing every original area.

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents an invalid value encountered while handling an archive selection.

#### Requirement: ReportInvalidValue

The system SHALL the system shall expose an invalid value as a valueerror.

#### Scenario: InvalidValueEncountered
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An input value is invalid for the requested operation.
- **WHEN** The operation reports the input failure.
- **THEN** A ValueError is produced.

### Sub-component: NextItem

> Implements: `next`

Retrieves the next item from an iterator, using a supplied fallback when available.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNextItem

The system SHALL the system shall return the next available item from the iterator or the supplied fallback when the iterator is exhausted.

#### Scenario: IteratorHasItem
- **GIVEN** The iterator contains another item.
- **WHEN** The next item is requested.
- **THEN** The next item is returned.

#### Scenario: IteratorExhaustedWithFallback
- **GIVEN** The iterator is exhausted and a fallback is supplied.
- **WHEN** The next item is requested.
- **THEN** The fallback is returned.

- **WHEN** The records are loaded.
- **THEN** The loaded records are made available.

- **THEN** Only rewards for newly reached levels are paid.

#### Scenario: ValidCLSMEntry
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player is eligible to enter CLSM
- **WHEN** clsm_entry is called
- **THEN** The service SHALL return the entry response

<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->

### Requirement: RequestFriendship

### Requirement: SearchPlayers

### Requirement: SuggestFriends

The system SHALL the service shall return suggested players who may be added as friends.

- **WHEN** tower_start is called

- **WHEN** tower_end is called

### Requirement: TowerRestart

- **WHEN** tower_restart is called
- **THEN** The service returns the restarted run state.

### Requirement: TowerRetire

The system SHALL the service shall provide the player's glory entry state and requirements.

The system SHALL the service shall start a survival run when its entry requirements are met.

The system SHALL the service shall restart a survival run when the request is valid.

- **GIVEN** A restartable survival run
- **WHEN** survival_restart is called
- **THEN** The service returns the restarted run state.


## Sub-components

> `Authored Running Modes Service` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Feature

> Implements: `_feature`

Determines whether an authored running mode feature is available for the current request.

#### Requirement: DetermineFeatureAvailability

The system SHALL the block must determine whether the requested running mode feature is available from the supplied context.

#### Scenario: FeatureAvailable
- **GIVEN** The context enables the requested feature
- **WHEN** Feature availability is evaluated
- **THEN** The block reports the feature as available

### Sub-component: Finish

> Implements: `_finish`

Completes an active authored running mode and records its completion state.

#### Requirement: CompleteRunningMode

The system SHALL the block shall complete the active running mode and produce the corresponding completion outcome.

#### Scenario: ActiveModeFinished
- **GIVEN** A running mode is active
- **WHEN** The finish block is invoked
- **THEN** The mode is marked complete and a completion outcome is produced

### Sub-component: Missions

> Implements: `_missions`

Processes mission-related state for authored running modes.

#### Requirement: ProcessMissionState

The system SHALL the block shall process mission state relevant to the current running mode.

#### Scenario: MissionStateProcessed
- **GIVEN** The running mode has mission state
- **WHEN** Mission processing is invoked
- **THEN** The relevant mission state is processed

### Sub-component: Next

> Implements: `next`

Advances an authored running mode to its next state or step.

#### Requirement: AdvanceMode

The system SHALL the block must advance the running mode when a valid next step is available.

#### Scenario: NextStepAvailable
- **GIVEN** The running mode has a valid next step
- **WHEN** The next block is invoked
- **THEN** The mode advances to that step

### Sub-component: Tower

> Implements: `_tower`

Handles tower-mode state and progression.

#### Requirement: ProcessTowerMode

The system SHALL the block shall process the tower mode state and its progression.

#### Scenario: TowerProgression
- **GIVEN** A tower mode is active
- **WHEN** Tower processing is invoked
- **THEN** The tower state is processed for progression

### Sub-component: Save

> Implements: `_save`

Persists the supplied model state.

#### Requirement: SaveModel

The system SHALL the block must persist the supplied model.

#### Scenario: ModelSaved
- **GIVEN** A model containing running mode state is supplied
- **WHEN** _save is invoked
- **THEN** The model state is persisted

### Sub-component: Refresh

> Implements: `_refresh`

Refreshes derived or current state for an authored running mode.

#### Requirement: RefreshModeState

The system SHALL the block should refresh the running mode state before it is used further.

#### Scenario: StateRefreshed
- **GIVEN** Running mode state may be stale
- **WHEN** Refresh is invoked
- **THEN** The state available to later processing is refreshed

### Sub-component: Reply

> Implements: `_reply`

Produces a response for the current authored running mode operation.

#### Requirement: ProduceReply

The system SHALL the block must produce a reply that reflects the outcome of the current operation.

#### Scenario: OperationReplyProduced
- **GIVEN** A running mode operation has an outcome
- **WHEN** Reply generation is invoked
- **THEN** A reply describing that outcome is produced

### Sub-component: Deepcopy

> Implements: `deepcopy`

Creates an independent deep copy of a value used by running mode processing.

#### Requirement: CopyValue

The system SHALL the block shall return a copy whose nested mutable values are independent of the source.

#### Scenario: NestedValueCopied
- **GIVEN** A value contains nested mutable data
- **WHEN** A deep copy is made
- **THEN** Changes to the copy do not alter the source

### Sub-component: Setdefault

> Implements: `f.setdefault`

Provides a default value for a missing field in a mapping.

#### Requirement: EnsureDefaultField

The system SHALL the block must provide the requested default when the field is missing and preserve an existing field value.

#### Scenario: MissingFieldGetsDefault
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The mapping does not contain the requested field
- **WHEN** A default is requested
- **THEN** The field is set to the default value

### Sub-component: Node

> Implements: `_node`

Resolves a running mode node from the relevant mode state.

#### Requirement: ResolveModeNode

The system SHALL the block shall resolve the node associated with the current mode state.

#### Scenario: NodeResolved
- **GIVEN** Mode state references a node
- **WHEN** Node resolution is invoked
- **THEN** The referenced node is returned

### Sub-component: Mission

> Implements: `_mission`

Resolves or processes a mission for the current running mode.

#### Requirement: ResolveMission

The system SHALL the block shall identify the mission associated with the current mode operation.

#### Scenario: MissionIdentified
- **GIVEN** The mode state references a mission
- **WHEN** Mission resolution is invoked
- **THEN** The referenced mission is identified

### Sub-component: Glory

> Implements: `_glory`

Handles glory-mode state and progression.

#### Requirement: ProcessGloryMode

The system SHALL the block shall process glory mode state for the current operation.

#### Scenario: GloryStateProcessed
- **GIVEN** Glory mode is active
- **WHEN** Glory processing is invoked
- **THEN** Glory mode state is processed

### Sub-component: Need

> Implements: `_need`

Determines or enforces a required prerequisite for a running mode operation.

#### Requirement: CheckRequirement

The system SHALL the block must determine whether the required prerequisite is satisfied.

#### Scenario: RequirementSatisfied
- **GIVEN** The required prerequisite is present
- **WHEN** The requirement is checked
- **THEN** The block recognizes that the prerequisite is satisfied

### Sub-component: Csv

> Implements: `_csv`

Converts or interprets comma-separated data used by running mode processing.

#### Requirement: ProcessCsvValue

The system SHALL the block shall process comma-separated input into its constituent values.

#### Scenario: MultipleCsvValues
- **GIVEN** Input contains multiple comma-separated values
- **WHEN** CSV processing is invoked
- **THEN** The constituent values are returned

### Sub-component: Mst

> Implements: `_mst`

Resolves master data relevant to an authored running mode.

#### Requirement: ResolveMasterData

The system SHALL the block shall provide the master data required by the current mode operation.

#### Scenario: MasterDataAvailable
- **GIVEN** The requested mode data exists
- **WHEN** Master data is resolved
- **THEN** The relevant master data is returned

### Sub-component: Any

> Implements: `any`

Determines whether at least one item in an iterable satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAnyMatch

The system SHALL the block must report whether any item satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: MatchingItemExists
- **GIVEN** At least one item satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The iterable is checked
- **THEN** The result indicates a match

### Sub-component: Begin

> Implements: `_begin`

Begins an authored running mode and establishes its initial state.

#### Requirement: BeginRunningMode

The system SHALL the block shall initialize the running mode when a valid start request is received.

#### Scenario: ModeBegins
- **GIVEN** A valid request to start a mode is received
- **WHEN** Begin processing is invoked
- **THEN** Initial running mode state is established

### Sub-component: Resume

> Implements: `_resume`

Resumes a previously started authored running mode.

#### Requirement: ResumeRunningMode

The system SHALL the block must resume an eligible previously started mode from its saved state.

#### Scenario: SavedModeResumed
- **GIVEN** A previously started mode has resumable saved state
- **WHEN** Resume processing is invoked
- **THEN** The mode continues from its saved state

### Sub-component: Rng

> Implements: `_rng`

Provides random selection behavior required by running mode processing.

#### Requirement: SelectRandomValue

The system SHALL the block shall provide a random selection from the eligible values.

#### Scenario: EligibleValuesPresent
- **GIVEN** One or more eligible values are available
- **WHEN** Random selection is requested
- **THEN** A value from the eligible set is returned

### Sub-component: GlorySelection

> Implements: `_glory_selection`

Selects the glory-mode option or content applicable to the current state.

#### Requirement: SelectGloryContent

The system SHALL the block must select glory content eligible for the current running mode state.

#### Scenario: EligibleGloryContentSelected
- **GIVEN** Eligible glory content exists
- **WHEN** Glory selection is invoked
- **THEN** An eligible option is selected

### Sub-component: OperationGet

> Implements: `op.get`

Reads a value from an operation mapping with an optional default.

#### Requirement: ReadOperationField

The system SHALL the block shall return the requested operation field or its default when absent.

#### Scenario: AbsentFieldUsesDefault
- **GIVEN** The operation mapping lacks the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** The supplied default is returned

### Sub-component: ChoiceGet

> Implements: `choice.get`

Reads a value from a choice mapping with an optional default.

#### Requirement: ReadChoiceField

The system SHALL the block shall return the requested choice field or its default when absent.

#### Scenario: ChoiceFieldRead
- **GIVEN** A choice mapping contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** The field value is returned

### Sub-component: Get

> Implements: `f.get`

Reads a value from a mapping with an optional default.

#### Requirement: ReadMappingField

The system SHALL the block must return the requested field value or the supplied default when the field is absent.

#### Scenario: MappingFieldAbsent
- **GIVEN** The mapping lacks the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** The supplied default is returned

### Sub-component: Update

> Implements: `out.update`

Adds or replaces fields in the current output mapping.

#### Requirement: UpdateOutput

The system SHALL the block shall add supplied fields to the output mapping and replace existing values for matching keys.

#### Scenario: OutputFieldUpdated
- **GIVEN** The output mapping contains a field with an older value
- **WHEN** An update supplies a new value for that field
- **THEN** The output mapping contains the new value

### Sub-component: MissionResultFrom

> Implements: `mission_result_from`

Parses the client's mission-end report into structured context for condition evaluators.

#### Requirement: ParseMissionEndReport

The system SHALL the block must interpret the client's mission-end report as the context consumed by condition evaluators.

#### Scenario: ValidReportParsed
- **GIVEN** Plaintext contains a valid mission-end report
- **WHEN** mission_result_from is called
- **THEN** A dictionary representing the report context is returned

#### Scenario: MissingReport
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Plaintext is absent
- **WHEN** mission_result_from is called
- **THEN** A dictionary result is returned without requiring report content

### Sub-component: CarryReply

> Implements: `_carry_reply`

Produces a reply describing carry-over state for a running mode.

#### Requirement: ReplyWithCarryState

The system SHALL the block shall produce a reply that communicates the applicable carry-over state.

#### Scenario: CarryStateIncluded
- **GIVEN** Carry-over state is available
- **WHEN** The carry reply is generated
- **THEN** The reply reflects the carry-over state

### Sub-component: Dumps

> Implements: `json.dumps`

Serializes a value into JSON text.

#### Requirement: SerializeJson

The system SHALL the block must serialize the supplied json-compatible value into json text.

#### Scenario: MappingSerialized
- **GIVEN** A JSON-compatible mapping is supplied
- **WHEN** JSON serialization is performed
- **THEN** JSON text representing the mapping is returned

### Sub-component: ContextGet

> Implements: `context.get`

Reads a value from the evaluator context with an optional default.

#### Requirement: ReadContextField

The system SHALL the block shall return the requested context field or its default when absent.

#### Scenario: ContextFieldAvailable
- **GIVEN** The context contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** The field value is returned

### Sub-component: RunGet

> Implements: `run.get`

Reads a value from a running mode record with an optional default.

#### Requirement: ReadRunField

The system SHALL the block must return the requested run field or the default when absent.

#### Scenario: RunFieldAvailable
- **GIVEN** The run record contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** The field value is returned

### Sub-component: MetricsGet

> Implements: `metrics.get`

Reads a value from mode metrics with an optional default.

The system SHALL the block shall return the requested metric or its default when absent.

#### Scenario: MetricMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The metrics mapping lacks the requested metric
- **WHEN** The metric is read
- **THEN** The supplied default is returned

### Sub-component: UserGet

> Implements: `user.get`

Reads a value from user state with an optional default.

#### Requirement: ReadUserField

The system SHALL the block shall return the requested user field or its default when absent.

#### Scenario: UserFieldRead
- **GIVEN** User state contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** The field value is returned

### Sub-component: UnlockedAdd

> Implements: `unlocked.add`

Marks an item as unlocked in the current running mode state.

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied item to the set of unlocked items.

#### Scenario: NewItemUnlocked

- **WHEN** It is added to the unlocked set
- **THEN** The item is present in the unlocked set

### Sub-component: NodeGet

> Implements: `node.get`

Reads a value from a running mode node with an optional default.

- **GIVEN** The node contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** The field value is returned

Handles survival-mode state and progression.

#### Scenario: SurvivalModeProcessed
- **GIVEN** Survival mode is active
- **WHEN** Survival processing is invoked
- **THEN** Survival mode state is processed

### Sub-component: All

> Implements: `all`

Determines whether every item in an iterable satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAllMatch

The system SHALL the block must report whether every item satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: AllItemsMatch
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Every item satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The iterable is checked
- **THEN** The result indicates success

### Sub-component: PartyGet

> Implements: `party.get`

Reads a value from party state with an optional default.

#### Requirement: ReadPartyField

The system SHALL the block shall return the requested party field or its default when absent.

#### Scenario: PartyFieldAvailable
- **GIVEN** Party state contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** The field value is returned

### Sub-component: PackedStartsWith

Checks whether packed text begins with a specified prefix.

- **WHEN** The prefix is checked
- **THEN** The result indicates a match

Splits text into parts using a specified separator.

- **THEN** The resulting parts are returned

Splits packed text into its constituent parts.

Provides helper behavior required by authored running mode operations.

The system SHALL the block shall perform the helper action required by the current mode operation.

- **GIVEN** A mode operation requires helper processing
- **WHEN** The helper block is invoked
- **THEN** The required helper action is handled

### Sub-component: RmSnapshot

> Implements: `_rm_snapshot`

Creates a snapshot of running mode state.
