<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Running mode state exists
- **WHEN** A snapshot is requested
- **THEN** A snapshot of the current state is produced

### Sub-component: Rm

> Implements: `_rm`

Resolves or processes running mode state.

#### Requirement: ProcessRunningModeState

The system SHALL the block shall process the current running mode state.

#### Scenario: RunningModeStateHandled
- **GIVEN** Running mode state is available
- **WHEN** The block is invoked
- **THEN** The current state is handled

### Sub-component: Update

> Implements: `f.update`

Adds or replaces fields in a mapping.

#### Requirement: UpdateMapping

The system SHALL the block shall add supplied fields to the mapping and replace values for matching keys.

#### Scenario: MappingUpdated
- **GIVEN** The mapping has an existing field
- **WHEN** An update supplies a replacement
- **THEN** The field contains the replacement value

### Sub-component: Carry

> Implements: `_carry`

Processes carry-over state between running mode steps.

#### Requirement: ProcessCarryState

The system SHALL the block must process state carried forward from a prior running mode step.

#### Scenario: CarryStateApplied
- **GIVEN** Prior state is eligible to carry forward
- **WHEN** Carry processing is invoked
- **THEN** The eligible state is carried into the current step

### Sub-component: Retire

> Implements: `_retire`

Retires an authored running mode and handles its terminal state.

#### Requirement: RetireRunningMode

The system SHALL the block shall retire the running mode when retirement is requested.

#### Scenario: ModeRetired
- **GIVEN** A running mode is eligible for retirement
- **WHEN** Retirement is processed
- **THEN** The mode is placed in its retired state

### Sub-component: Tfa

> Implements: `_tfa`

Handles TFA-related running mode state or rewards.

#### Requirement: ProcessTfa

The system SHALL the block shall process the tfa content relevant to the current running mode operation.

#### Scenario: TfaContentProcessed
- **GIVEN** TFA state or content is available
- **WHEN** TFA processing is invoked
- **THEN** The relevant TFA content is processed

### Sub-component: Number

> Implements: `_number`

Interprets a value as a number for running mode processing.

#### Requirement: ParseNumber

The system SHALL the block must produce the numeric interpretation of a valid numeric input.

#### Scenario: ValidNumber
- **GIVEN** A valid numeric value is supplied
- **WHEN** Numeric interpretation is requested
- **THEN** The corresponding number is returned

### Sub-component: GambitBonuses

> Implements: `gambit_bonuses`

Returns gambit bonuses applicable to an area, tier, and selected options.

#### Requirement: ResolveGambitBonuses

The system SHALL the block shall determine gambit bonuses for the supplied area, tier, and selection.

#### Scenario: MatchingBonusesFound
- **GIVEN** The catalogue contains bonuses matching the supplied area, tier, and selection
- **WHEN** gambit_bonuses is called
- **THEN** The matching bonuses are returned

### Sub-component: Refused

> Implements: `_Refused`

Represents a refusal or rejected running mode operation.

#### Requirement: SignalRefusal

The system SHALL the block must represent refusal as a value error condition.

#### Scenario: OperationRefused
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A requested operation cannot be accepted
- **WHEN** The refusal condition is raised
- **THEN** A _Refused error is produced

### Sub-component: RowGet

> Implements: `row.get`

Reads a value from a row mapping with an optional default.

#### Requirement: ReadRowField

The system SHALL the block shall return the requested row field or its default when absent.

#### Scenario: RowFieldAvailable
- **GIVEN** The row contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** The row field value is returned

### Sub-component: ProducedUpdate

> Implements: `produced.update`

Adds or replaces fields in a produced result mapping.

#### Requirement: UpdateProducedResult

The system SHALL the block must add supplied fields to the produced result and replace existing values for matching keys.

#### Scenario: ProducedResultUpdated
- **GIVEN** The produced result contains an existing field
- **WHEN** An update supplies a replacement
- **THEN** The new value is present in the produced result

### Sub-component: TfaItems

> Implements: `_tfa_items`

Provides the TFA items relevant to running mode processing.

#### Requirement: ListTfaItems

The system SHALL the block shall provide the applicable tfa items.

#### Scenario: ItemsAvailable
- **GIVEN** TFA items are available
- **WHEN** The item list is requested
- **THEN** The applicable items are returned

### Sub-component: TfaCapacity

> Implements: `_tfa_capacity`

Determines capacity for TFA items.

#### Requirement: DetermineTfaCapacity

The system SHALL the block must determine the capacity available for tfa items.

#### Scenario: CapacityDetermined
- **GIVEN** The current TFA state is available
- **WHEN** Capacity is requested
- **THEN** The available capacity is returned

### Sub-component: TfaPack

> Implements: `_tfa_pack`

Packages TFA data for running mode state or output.

#### Requirement: PackTfaData

The system SHALL the block shall package applicable tfa data for consumption by the running mode flow.

#### Scenario: TfaDataPackaged
- **GIVEN** TFA data is available
- **WHEN** Packaging is requested
- **THEN** A packaged representation is returned

### Sub-component: Grant

> Implements: `_grant`

Grants an applicable item or reward to the running mode participant.

#### Requirement: GrantReward

The system SHALL the block must grant the requested eligible item or reward.

#### Scenario: EligibleRewardGranted
- **GIVEN** An eligible reward is available
- **WHEN** The grant block is invoked
- **THEN** The reward is granted

### Sub-component: ChoiceSplit

> Implements: `choice.split`

Splits choice text into constituent values.

#### Requirement: SplitChoice

The system SHALL the block shall return the values obtained by splitting the choice text.

#### Scenario: ChoiceContainsSeparator
- **GIVEN** Choice text contains the separator
- **WHEN** The choice is split
- **THEN** Its constituent values are returned

### Sub-component: Fullmatch

> Implements: `re.fullmatch`

Checks whether an input string fully matches a regular expression.

#### Requirement: MatchEntireString

The system SHALL the block must indicate whether the entire input matches the supplied pattern.

#### Scenario: EntireStringMatches
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The full input matches the pattern
- **WHEN** The match is evaluated
- **THEN** A successful match is returned

### Sub-component: Groups

> Implements: `old.groups`

Returns the captured groups from a prior regular expression match.

#### Requirement: GetCapturedGroups

The system SHALL the block shall return the groups captured by the match.

#### Scenario: CapturesReturned
- **GIVEN** A match contains captured groups
- **WHEN** The groups are requested
- **THEN** The captured values are returned

### Sub-component: Extend

> Implements: `slots.extend`

Appends multiple values to the current slots collection.

#### Requirement: ExtendSlots

The system SHALL the block must append each supplied value to the slots collection.

#### Scenario: MultipleSlotsAdded
- **GIVEN** The slots collection is initialized
- **WHEN** Multiple values are extended into it
- **THEN** All supplied values are present in the collection

### Sub-component: Groups

> Implements: `groups`

Builds the groups of player entities and related equipment, items, and progression data for a banner.

#### Requirement: BuildPlayerGroups

The system SHALL the block shall construct the player groups and associated category data for the supplied player and banner.

#### Scenario: PlayerGroupsProduced
- **GIVEN** A player and banner are supplied
- **WHEN** groups is called
- **THEN** The corresponding grouped player data is returned

### Sub-component: Preview

> Implements: `preview`

Creates a preview of applicable items and groups for the requested tier and bonus values.

#### Requirement: CreateModePreview

The system SHALL the block shall produce a preview using the supplied tier and bonus values, applying defaults when omitted.

#### Scenario: PreviewProducedWithDefaults
- **GIVEN** No tier or bonus values are supplied
- **WHEN** preview is called
- **THEN** Preview data is produced using the default bonus values

> `source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

### Requirement: SellItems

The system SHALL the service shall validate a sale and settle it atomically against the player's inventory, crediting the appropriate proceeds.

#### Scenario: ValidItemSale
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player owns the requested quantity of sellable items
- **WHEN** item_sell is called
- **THEN** The items are removed and the proceeds are credited atomically

#### Scenario: InsufficientInventory
- **GIVEN** The player does not own the requested quantity
- **WHEN** item_sell is called
- **THEN** The sale is refused without partially changing inventory or currency

### Requirement: BuyItems

The system SHALL the service shall validate a purchase, collect its cost, grant its rewards, and return the purchase result.

- **Implementation**: `item_buy::source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

#### Scenario: ValidPurchase
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested product is available and the player can pay its cost
- **WHEN** item_buy is called
- **THEN** The cost is collected and the purchased rewards are granted

#### Scenario: PurchaseCannotBeCompleted
- **GIVEN** The product is unavailable or the player cannot meet its requirements
- **WHEN** item_buy is called
- **THEN** The request is refused without granting the purchase

### Requirement: OpenStrongbox

The system SHALL the service shall validate a strongbox opening request, collect its costs, and grant the corresponding rewards.

- **Implementation**: `strongbox_open::source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

#### Scenario: EligibleStrongboxOpening
- **GIVEN** The player meets the opening requirements and owns the required costs
- **WHEN** strongbox_open is called
- **THEN** The costs are collected and the strongbox rewards are granted

#### Scenario: OpeningRequirementsNotMet
- **GIVEN** The player lacks a required cost or eligibility condition
- **WHEN** strongbox_open is called
- **THEN** The request is refused without consuming costs

### Requirement: ExchangeShopPurchase

The system SHALL the service shall exchange eligible player resources for the requested shop rewards.

- **Implementation**: `exchange_shop::source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

#### Scenario: EligibleExchange
- **GIVEN** The player has the required exchange resources
- **WHEN** exchange_shop is called
- **THEN** The costs are collected and the configured rewards are granted

#### Scenario: InsufficientExchangeResources
- **GIVEN** The player lacks the required resources
- **WHEN** exchange_shop is called
- **THEN** The exchange is refused

### Requirement: ListShopExchanges

The system SHALL the service shall return available exchange shop entries based on recovered stock schedules and feature availability.

- **Implementation**: `shop_exchange_list::source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

#### Scenario: ShopEntriesAvailable
- **GIVEN** Exchange stock is configured and its feature is available
- **WHEN** shop_exchange_list is called
- **THEN** The response includes the available exchange entries

### Requirement: PurchaseShopExchangeItem

The system SHALL the service shall validate an exchange item purchase, collect its costs, grant its rewards, and update applicable exchange state.

- **Implementation**: `shop_exchange_item::source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

#### Scenario: ValidExchangeItemPurchase
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested entry is available and the player meets its requirements
- **WHEN** shop_exchange_item is called
- **THEN** The costs are collected, rewards are granted, and exchange state is updated

#### Scenario: ExchangePurchaseIsIneligible
- **GIVEN** The entry is unavailable or the player does not meet its requirements
- **WHEN** shop_exchange_item is called
- **THEN** The request is refused without granting rewards

### Requirement: ExchangeUnits

The system SHALL the service shall validate a unit exchange against player ownership and deployment constraints, then apply the exchange and its rewards.

- **Implementation**: `shop_exchange_unit::source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

#### Scenario: EligibleUnitExchange
- **GIVEN** The requested units satisfy exchange requirements and are not blocked by deployment constraints
- **WHEN** shop_exchange_unit is called
- **THEN** The exchange is applied and configured rewards are granted

#### Scenario: UnitExchangeIsBlocked
- **GIVEN** A required unit is missing or cannot be exchanged due to deployment constraints
- **WHEN** shop_exchange_unit is called
- **THEN** The request is refused without dropping units

### Requirement: CheckVariableStoreAvailability

The system SHALL the service shall determine whether a variable store is available for the request and return its status.

- **Implementation**: `variable_store_check::source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

#### Scenario: StoreStatusRequested
- **GIVEN** A variable store is configured
- **WHEN** variable_store_check is called
- **THEN** The response reports the store's applicable availability

### Requirement: LoadArchivedStoreSchedules

The system SHALL the service shall provide archived exchange stock in the native store schedule format.

- **Implementation**: `archived_store_schedules::source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

#### Scenario: ArchivedSchedulesAvailable
- **GIVEN** Recovered exchange stock data exists
- **WHEN** archived_store_schedules is called
- **THEN** The schedules are returned in the native store schedule format

### Requirement: MedalExchange

The system SHALL the service shall validate a medal exchange, collect its costs, grant configured rewards, and persist applicable exchange state.

- **Implementation**: `medal_exchange::source/server/exvius_actions_shops.py`

#### Scenario: ValidMedalExchange
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player meets the exchange requirements and has sufficient medals
- **WHEN** medal_exchange is called
- **THEN** Medals are collected and configured rewards are granted

#### Scenario: InvalidOrUnaffordableExchange
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested exchange is invalid or the player lacks required medals
- **WHEN** medal_exchange is called
- **THEN** The request is refused without granting rewards


## Sub-components

> `Shop and Item Service` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: NodeLookup

> Implements: `_node`

Resolve an orchestrator node from the available node definitions.

#### Requirement: ResolveNode

The system SHALL the block shall resolve the requested node when it exists.

#### Scenario: NodeExists
- **GIVEN** A requested node is present in the node definitions
- **WHEN** The block resolves that node
- **THEN** The matching node is returned

### Sub-component: RequiredValue

> Implements: `_require`

Require a necessary value and report when it is absent.

#### Requirement: RequireValue

The system SHALL the block must provide the required value or signal that it is missing.

#### Scenario: ValueMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A required value is absent
- **WHEN** The block checks for it
- **THEN** The missing-value condition is reported

### Sub-component: Triples

> Implements: `_triples`

Extract or normalize triples from supplied data.

#### Requirement: ExtractTriples

The system SHALL the block shall return the triples represented by its input.

#### Scenario: TriplesPresent
- **GIVEN** Input data contains triples
- **WHEN** The block processes the data
- **THEN** Those triples are returned

### Sub-component: ProductLookup

> Implements: `_product`

Resolve a product from the available product data.

#### Requirement: ResolveProduct

The system SHALL the block shall return the product matching the requested identifier when present.

#### Scenario: ProductFound
- **GIVEN** A matching product exists
- **WHEN** The block resolves the product
- **THEN** The matching product is returned

### Sub-component: IntegerConversion

> Implements: `_int`

Convert a value to an integer representation.

#### Requirement: ConvertInteger

The system SHALL the block shall convert a supported value to an integer.

#### Scenario: NumericInput
- **GIVEN** A value represents an integer
- **WHEN** The block converts it
- **THEN** The corresponding integer is returned

### Sub-component: Counter

> Implements: `Counter`

Count occurrences of values in an input collection.

#### Requirement: CountValues

The system SHALL the block shall provide occurrence counts for input values.

#### Scenario: RepeatedValues
- **GIVEN** An input collection contains repeated values
- **WHEN** Counts are constructed
- **THEN** Each value has its occurrence count

### Sub-component: IteratorNext

> Implements: `next`

Retrieve the next value from an iterator.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNext

The system SHALL the block shall return the next available iterator value.

#### Scenario: ValueAvailable
- **GIVEN** An iterator has a next value
- **WHEN** The block advances it
- **THEN** That value is returned

### Sub-component: Reply

> Implements: `_reply`

Construct a response for an orchestrator request.

#### Requirement: CreateReply

The system SHALL the block shall produce a response reflecting the supplied result.

#### Scenario: ResultProvided
- **GIVEN** A request result is available
- **WHEN** The block creates a reply
- **THEN** A response containing that result is returned

### Sub-component: NodeGet

> Implements: `node.get`

Retrieve a value from a node mapping.

#### Requirement: GetNodeValue

The system SHALL the block shall return the mapped value for a present key.

#### Scenario: KeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The node mapping contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

Retrieve a value from a product mapping.

The system SHALL the block shall return the mapped value for a present key.

- **THEN** The associated value is returned

- **THEN** The player receives the reward

Retrieve a value from the mapping n.

- **GIVEN** A CSV value contains valid reward entries
- **WHEN** It is parsed
- **THEN** The reward entries are returned

### Sub-component: BonusesExtend

> Implements: `bonuses.extend`

Add multiple bonuses to the bonuses collection.

#### Requirement: ExtendBonuses

The system SHALL the block shall add all supplied bonuses to the collection.

#### Scenario: BonusesSupplied
- **GIVEN** A sequence of bonuses is supplied
- **WHEN** The collection is extended
- **THEN** All bonuses are present

### Sub-component: ResultsAppend

> Implements: `results.append`

Add a value to the results collection.

#### Requirement: AppendResultEntry

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied value to the results collection.

#### Scenario: ResultSupplied
- **GIVEN** A result value is supplied
- **WHEN** It is appended
- **THEN** The results collection contains it

### Sub-component: CompletedAppend

> Implements: `completed.append`

Add an entry to the completed collection.

#### Requirement: AppendCompleted

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied entry to the completed collection.

#### Scenario: CompletedEntry
- **GIVEN** A completed entry is identified
- **WHEN** It is appended
- **THEN** The collection contains the entry

### Sub-component: Slot

> Implements: `_slot`

Resolve slot data for a service or shop context.

#### Requirement: ResolveSlot

The system SHALL the block shall return slot data corresponding to the supplied context.

#### Scenario: SlotAvailable
- **GIVEN** Slot data exists for the context
- **WHEN** The slot is resolved
- **THEN** The corresponding data is returned

### Sub-component: SlotGet

> Implements: `slot.get`

Retrieve a value from a slot mapping.

#### Requirement: GetSlotEntry

The system SHALL the block shall return the mapped value for a present slot key.

#### Scenario: KeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The slot mapping contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: TimeNow

> Implements: `time.time`

Read the current system time as a timestamp.

#### Requirement: ReadCurrentTime

The system SHALL the block shall provide the current system timestamp.

#### Scenario: TimeRequested
- **GIVEN** The current time is requested
- **WHEN** The block reads the clock
- **THEN** A current timestamp is returned

### Sub-component: SlotUpdate

> Implements: `slot.update`

Update a slot mapping with supplied values.

#### Requirement: UpdateSlot

The system SHALL the block shall apply supplied entries to the slot mapping.

#### Scenario: SlotValuesSupplied
- **GIVEN** Slot values are supplied
- **WHEN** The mapping is updated
- **THEN** The mapping reflects those values

### Sub-component: CraftCharge

> Implements: `_craft_charge`

Determine the charge required for a crafting operation.

#### Requirement: CalculateCraftCharge

The system SHALL the block shall return the charge required by the supplied crafting context.

#### Scenario: ChargeDefined
- **GIVEN** A crafting operation has a defined charge
- **WHEN** The charge is determined
- **THEN** The required charge is returned

### Sub-component: LoadFriends

> Implements: `load_friends`

Load friend profiles from a file, refreshing cached data when the file changes.

#### Requirement: LoadFriendProfiles

The system SHALL the block shall return valid friend entries from the configured file, treating a missing file as an empty friend list.

#### Scenario: FriendFileMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The configured friends file does not exist
- **WHEN** Friend profiles are loaded
- **THEN** An empty friend list is returned without an error

### Sub-component: Receipt

> Implements: `receipt`

Create or retrieve a receipt for a completed transaction.

#### Requirement: ResolveReceipt

The system SHALL the block shall provide a receipt associated with the transaction.

#### Scenario: TransactionCompleted
- **GIVEN** A transaction has completed
- **WHEN** Its receipt is requested
- **THEN** A receipt for the transaction is returned

### Sub-component: SatisfiedQuests

> Implements: `_satisfied_quests`

Determine which quests are satisfied by current progress.

#### Requirement: ListSatisfiedQuests

The system SHALL the block shall return quests whose requirements are satisfied.

#### Scenario: QuestRequirementsMet
- **GIVEN** Progress meets a quest's requirements
- **WHEN** Satisfied quests are determined
- **THEN** That quest is included

### Sub-component: DailyQuestsForAction

> Implements: `_daily_quests_for_action`

Resolve daily quests relevant to a specified action.

#### Requirement: FindDailyQuestsForAction

The system SHALL the block shall return daily quests that match the supplied action.

#### Scenario: ActionMatchesQuest
- **GIVEN** A daily quest applies to the action
- **WHEN** Relevant quests are resolved
- **THEN** The matching quest is returned

### Sub-component: TimeStrftime

> Implements: `time.strftime`

Format a time value according to a format string.

#### Requirement: FormatTime

The system SHALL the block shall return a formatted representation of the supplied time.

#### Scenario: ValidFormat
- **GIVEN** A supported time format and time value are supplied
- **WHEN** The time is formatted
- **THEN** A formatted string is returned

### Sub-component: TimeGmtime

> Implements: `time.gmtime`

Convert a timestamp to a UTC time representation.

#### Requirement: ConvertToUtcTime

The system SHALL the block shall return the utc time representation for the supplied timestamp.

#### Scenario: TimestampSupplied
- **GIVEN** A timestamp is supplied
- **WHEN** It is converted to UTC time
- **THEN** A UTC time representation is returned

### Sub-component: SentGet

Retrieve sent-message data by key.

#### Scenario: SentEntryPresent

- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

Retrieve gift data by key.

- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated gift is returned

### Sub-component: GiftGet

> Implements: `gift.get`

Retrieve a value from a gift mapping.

The system SHALL the block shall return the mapped value for a present gift key.

- **GIVEN** The gift mapping contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: RewardSplit

> Implements: `reward.split`

Separate a reward descriptor into components.

The system SHALL the block shall return components produced by splitting the reward descriptor.

#### Scenario: DelimiterPresent
- **GIVEN** A reward descriptor contains the delimiter
- **WHEN** It is split
- **THEN** Its components are returned

### Sub-component: GiftsValues

> Implements: `gifts.values`

Expose values recorded in the gifts mapping.

### Sub-component: ConvertMaterialUnits

> Implements: `convert_material_units`

Convert eligible material units and account for resulting credits, points, or currency.

#### Requirement: ConvertEligibleMaterials

The system SHALL the block shall convert eligible material units, account for resulting credits or points, and apply any resulting currency or unit changes.

#### Scenario: EligibleMaterialPresent
- **GIVEN** The player owns material units eligible for conversion
- **WHEN** Material conversion is processed
- **THEN** Eligible units are converted and resulting account changes are applied

### Sub-component: ArchiveRows

Produce archived shop or service rows from the supplied context.

#### Scenario: ArchiveRowsAvailable

- **WHEN** Archive rows are requested
- **THEN** The corresponding rows are returned

> Implements: `craft.get`

Retrieve crafting data by key.

#### Scenario: CraftEntryPresent
- **GIVEN** The craft mapping contains a requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated crafting value is returned

### Sub-component: CraftSetdefault

> Implements: `craft.setdefault`

Ensure the craft mapping contains a key, initializing it when absent.

#### Scenario: CraftKeyMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->

- **WHEN** A default is applied

> `source/server/exvius_actions.py`

### Requirement: UpdateOptions

The system SHALL the service shall save the fields shared by the native option request and response.

- **Implementation**: `option_update::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: SaveSupportedOptionFields
- **GIVEN** A request contains supported native option fields
- **WHEN** option_update is called
- **THEN** The corresponding option values are saved

### Requirement: BuildOptionsReply

The system SHALL the service shall produce the option response from the current option state.

- **Implementation**: `option_reply::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: ReturnCurrentOptions
- **GIVEN** Option values are available in the current state
- **WHEN** option_reply is called
- **THEN** The reply contains the option values

### Requirement: SellItem

The system SHALL the service shall sell an item through the shop transaction path and account for equipment ownership separately.

#### Scenario: SellAnOwnedItem
- **GIVEN** The player owns an item eligible for sale
- **WHEN** item_sell is called with the sale request
- **THEN** The shop transaction is applied and ownership is updated

### Requirement: RestartMission

The system SHALL the service shall continue a mission by charging the configured continue cost.

- **Implementation**: `mission_restart::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: ContinueAMission
- **GIVEN** The player can pay the configured continue cost
- **WHEN** mission_restart is called
- **THEN** The continue cost is spent and the mission is restarted

### Requirement: AwakenUnit

The system SHALL the service shall apply an eligible unit awakening using the configured awakening requirements and rewards.

- **Implementation**: `unit_class_up::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: CompleteAnEligibleAwakening
- **GIVEN** The base unit and required materials are available
- **WHEN** unit_class_up is called
- **THEN** The awakening is applied and the consumed instance is replaced as required

### Requirement: EquipUnit

The system SHALL the service shall update a unit's equipment and loadout while respecting owned equipment and materia.

- **Implementation**: `unit_equip::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: EquipOwnedGear
- **GIVEN** The request references equipment available to the player
- **WHEN** unit_equip is called
- **THEN** The unit loadout is updated and ownership constraints are preserved

### Requirement: SacrificeUnits

The system SHALL the service shall resolve fragment rewards for the full batch before consuming eligible units.

- **Implementation**: `sacrifice::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: SacrificeEligibleUnits
- **GIVEN** The requested units are eligible and their batch rewards can be resolved
- **WHEN** sacrifice is called
- **THEN** Rewards are resolved and credited before eligible units are consumed

### Requirement: UpdateProfile

The system SHALL the service shall apply supported profile changes from a player request.

- **Implementation**: `update_profile::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: SaveProfileChanges
- **GIVEN** A request contains supported profile updates
- **WHEN** update_profile is called
- **THEN** The player's profile reflects the requested changes

### Requirement: UpdateInventoryFavourites

The system SHALL the service shall mark and unmark inventory entries as favourites for the specified inventory group.

- **Implementation**: `inventory_favourite::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: ChangeFavouriteFlags
- **GIVEN** The request identifies inventory entries to mark and unmark
- **WHEN** inventory_favourite is called
- **THEN** The requested favourite flags are updated

### Requirement: UpdateNoticeState

The system SHALL the service shall update the player's read state for supported notices.

- **Implementation**: `notice_state::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: MarkNoticesAsRead
- **GIVEN** The request identifies supported notices
- **WHEN** notice_state is called
- **THEN** The corresponding notice read state is saved

### Requirement: ServeAction

The system SHALL the service shall dispatch an action request to its registered handler and produce the corresponding reply.

- **Implementation**: `serve_actions::source/server/exvius_actions.py`

#### Scenario: DispatchASupportedAction
- **GIVEN** The request corresponds to a registered action handler
- **WHEN** serve_actions is called
- **THEN** The matching handler processes the request and the reply is returned


## Sub-components

> `ExviusActions` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: NodeAccess

> Implements: `_node`

Retrieves the node associated with the current action.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNode

The system SHALL the block must retrieve the current action's node.

#### Scenario: NodeAvailable
- **GIVEN** The action has a node
- **WHEN** The block runs
- **THEN** The associated node is retrieved

### Sub-component: AdvanceAction

> Implements: `next`

Advances processing to the next action state.

#### Requirement: Advance

The system SHALL the block must advance action processing to the next state.

#### Scenario: NextStateAvailable
- **GIVEN** Action processing is active
- **WHEN** The block runs
- **THEN** Processing advances to the next state

### Sub-component: NoteAccess

> Implements: `_note`

Retrieves note data associated with the action.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNote

The system SHALL the block must retrieve the action's note data.

#### Scenario: NoteAvailable
- **GIVEN** The action has note data
- **WHEN** The block runs
- **THEN** The note data is retrieved

### Sub-component: MasterAccess

> Implements: `_mst`

Retrieves master data used to process the action.

#### Requirement: RetrieveMaster

The system SHALL the block must retrieve the master data required by the action.

#### Scenario: MasterDataAvailable
- **GIVEN** The action requires master data
- **WHEN** The block runs
- **THEN** The relevant master data is retrieved

### Sub-component: MatchingLookup

> Implements: `any`

Checks whether any candidate satisfies the action's matching condition.

#### Requirement: FindMatch

The system SHALL the block must determine whether any candidate satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: CandidateMatches
- **GIVEN** At least one candidate satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The block checks the candidates
- **THEN** It reports a match

### Sub-component: UnitMasterLookup

> Implements: `unit_mst`

Looks up unit master data used to resolve unit-specific action behavior.

#### Requirement: LoadUnitMaster

The system SHALL the block must load unit master data for the requested unit.

#### Scenario: UnitMasterRequested
- **GIVEN** A unit master record is requested
- **WHEN** The block performs the lookup
- **THEN** The corresponding unit master data is returned

### Sub-component: RefreshState

> Implements: `_refresh`

Refreshes state needed to continue processing the action.

#### Requirement: Refresh

The system SHALL the block must refresh the action's relevant state.

#### Scenario: StateNeedsRefresh
- **GIVEN** Action state is ready to refresh
- **WHEN** The block runs
- **THEN** The relevant state is refreshed

### Sub-component: NodeLookup

> Implements: `node.get`

Reads a value from the node data.

#### Requirement: GetNodeValue

The system SHALL the block must read the requested value from the node data.

#### Scenario: NodeContainsKey
- **GIVEN** The node contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The block reads the key
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: StepLookup

> Implements: `step.get`

Reads a value from the current step data.

#### Requirement: GetStepValue

The system SHALL the block must read the requested value from the step data.

#### Scenario: StepContainsKey
- **GIVEN** The step contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The block reads the key
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: ValueSplit

> Implements: `m.split`

Splits a value into its component parts.

#### Requirement: SplitValue

The system SHALL the block must split the value into components according to its delimiter.

#### Scenario: MultipleParts
- **GIVEN** The value contains the expected delimiter
- **WHEN** The block splits the value
- **THEN** Its component parts are returned

### Sub-component: MaterialsSplit

> Implements: `materials.split`

Separates material data into individual components.

#### Requirement: SplitMaterials

The system SHALL the block must split material data into its individual components.

#### Scenario: MultipleMaterials
- **GIVEN** Material data contains multiple components
- **WHEN** The block splits the data
- **THEN** Each component is returned separately

### Sub-component: MaterialCount

> Implements: `m.count`

Reads the requested material count.

#### Requirement: ReadMaterialCount

The system SHALL the block must read the count associated with the material data.

#### Scenario: CountAvailable
- **GIVEN** Material data includes a count
- **WHEN** The block reads the count
- **THEN** The material count is returned

### Sub-component: DestinationLookup

> Implements: `destination.get`

Reads the destination associated with the action.

#### Requirement: GetDestination

The system SHALL the block must retrieve the action's destination value.

#### Scenario: DestinationAvailable
- **GIVEN** A destination is specified
- **WHEN** The block reads it
- **THEN** The destination is returned

### Sub-component: OrdinaryAwakeningReplacement

> Implements: `test_ordinary_awakening_also_replaces_the_consumed_instance`

Verifies that ordinary awakening replaces the consumed unit instance.

#### Requirement: ReplaceConsumedInstance

The system SHALL the block must ensure ordinary awakening replaces the consumed unit instance.

#### Scenario: OrdinaryAwakening
- **GIVEN** An owned unit is consumed for ordinary awakening
- **WHEN** The unit is awakened
- **THEN** The consumed instance is replaced and the resulting roster state reflects the awakening

### Sub-component: WireLoadouts

> Implements: `_wire_loadouts`

Connects saved loadout data with the applicable units and equipment.

#### Requirement: WireLoadoutData

The system SHALL the block must associate loadout data with its applicable units and equipment.

#### Scenario: LoadoutAvailable
- **GIVEN** Saved loadout data and unit data are available
- **WHEN** The block runs
- **THEN** The loadout data is associated with the applicable units

### Sub-component: CounterConstruction

> Implements: `Counter`

Creates a counter for tracking occurrences or quantities.

#### Requirement: CreateCounter

The system SHALL the block must provide a counter for tracking values.

#### Scenario: CounterInitialized
- **GIVEN** Values need to be counted
- **WHEN** The block creates a counter
- **THEN** A counter is available to track occurrences

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Signals that an action value is invalid.

#### Requirement: ReportInvalidValue

The system SHALL the block must identify invalid action values as value errors.

#### Scenario: InvalidValue
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An action supplies an invalid value
- **WHEN** The block validates the value
- **THEN** A value error is reported

### Sub-component: RetainOwnedEquipment

> Implements: `_retain_owned_equipment`

Preserves equipment that remains owned when action state is updated.

#### Requirement: RetainOwnedItems

The system SHALL the block must preserve owned equipment in the resulting state.

#### Scenario: EquipmentStillOwned
- **GIVEN** Equipment is owned before the state update
- **WHEN** The block processes the update
- **THEN** The equipment remains represented as owned

### Sub-component: ScoreQuestAction

> Implements: `score_quest_action`

Scores a verified activity against the original quest definitions and updates matching progress.

#### Requirement: ScoreVerifiedActivity

The system SHALL the block must match a verified activity against quest definitions and update progress for matching quests.

#### Scenario: ActivityMatchesQuest
- **GIVEN** A verified activity matches an original quest definition
- **WHEN** The activity is scored
- **THEN** The matching quest progress is updated by the supplied amount

Reads a value from the action request.

- **WHEN** The block reads the key
- **THEN** The corresponding value is returned

- **WHEN** The block reads the items
- **THEN** The item entries are returned

> Implements: `fields.items`

Reads field entries from the action data.

Splits the values data into components.

### Sub-component: MasterLookup

> Implements: `master.get`

Reads a value from master data.

#### Requirement: GetMasterValue

The system SHALL the block must retrieve the requested value from master data.

#### Scenario: MasterContainsKey

> `source/server/exvius_arena.py`

### Requirement: MatchArenaOpponent

The system SHALL the service shall match a player with an eligible arena opponent and return the match details.

- **Implementation**: `matching::source/server/exvius_arena.py`

#### Scenario: EligibleOpponentFound
- **GIVEN** A player requests an arena match and eligible rivals are available
- **WHEN** matching is called
- **THEN** The service returns a match response with a selected rival

### Requirement: StartArenaBattle

The system SHALL the service shall start an arena battle using the player's party and selected arena node.

#### Scenario: ValidBattleStart
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player has a valid arena node and party
- **WHEN** start is called
- **THEN** The service records the active battle and returns a start response

### Requirement: ParseArenaRewardEntries

The system SHALL the service shall parse supported item triples and level-1 unit reward descriptors into reward entries.

- **Implementation**: `reward_entries::source/server/exvius_arena.py`

#### Scenario: ValidRewardDescriptors
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Reward text contains supported item triples or level-1 unit descriptors
- **WHEN** reward_entries is called
- **THEN** The service returns the parsed rewards

#### Scenario: InvalidRewardDescriptor
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Reward text contains a malformed descriptor
- **WHEN** reward_entries is called
- **THEN** The service rejects the invalid descriptor

### Requirement: EndArenaBattle

The system SHALL the service shall resolve an arena battle, calculate its result and rewards, and settle any eligible weekly reward.

#### Scenario: BattleCompletion
- **GIVEN** An active arena battle has a valid completion request
- **WHEN** end is called
- **THEN** The service records the result and returns the calculated rewards

#### Scenario: WeeklyBoundaryReached
- **GIVEN** The player participated in a week that has closed at the original Thursday 04:00 UTC boundary
- **WHEN** end is called
- **THEN** The service settles that week's reward once at the earned tier

### Requirement: RestartArenaSession

The system SHALL the service shall restart the player's arena session and return the updated session state.

- **Implementation**: `restart::source/server/exvius_arena.py`

#### Scenario: RestartRequested
- **GIVEN** A player has an arena session eligible for restart
- **WHEN** restart is called
- **THEN** The service resets the session state and returns a restart response

### Requirement: SettleWeeklyArenaReward

The system SHALL the service shall settle one played week's arena reward at the original thursday 04:00 utc boundary and prevent duplicate payment.

- **Implementation**: `settle_weekly_reward::source/server/exvius_arena.py`

#### Scenario: ClosedWeekWithParticipation
- **GIVEN** The week has closed and the player has eligible participation
- **WHEN** settle_weekly_reward is called
- **THEN** The original tier reward is credited or delivered by mail once

#### Scenario: WeekHasNotClosed
- **GIVEN** The current time is before the weekly settlement boundary
- **WHEN** settle_weekly_reward is called
- **THEN** No reward is settled


## Sub-components

> `Arena State Service` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Timedelta

> Implements: `timedelta`

Provides a time interval used when determining the weekly settlement boundary.

#### Requirement: CreateTimeInterval

The system SHALL the block shall represent the requested duration as a time interval.

#### Scenario: DurationIsRepresented
- **GIVEN** A valid duration is provided
- **WHEN** The interval is created
- **THEN** The resulting interval represents that duration

### Sub-component: MasterTime

> Implements: `_mst`

Provides the master time value used by arena state processing.

#### Requirement: ProvideMasterTime

The system SHALL the block shall provide the master time value required by its caller.

#### Scenario: MasterTimeIsAvailable
- **GIVEN** Arena state processing requires master time
- **WHEN** The master time block is called
- **THEN** A master time value is returned

### Sub-component: Parse

> Implements: `parse`

Parses raw unit reward descriptors into structured reward entries.

#### Requirement: ParseRewardDescriptors

The system SHALL the block shall parse valid raw reward descriptors into structured entries and shall reject malformed descriptors.

#### Scenario: ValidDescriptorsAreParsed
- **GIVEN** Raw text contains valid unit reward descriptors
- **WHEN** The text is parsed
- **THEN** The corresponding structured entries are returned

#### Scenario: MalformedDescriptorIsRejected
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Raw text contains an invalid or non-ASCII numeric field
- **WHEN** The text is parsed
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised

### Sub-component: All

> Implements: `all`

Determines whether every element in an iterable satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAllElements

The system SHALL the block shall report whether all elements satisfy the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: AllElementsSatisfyCondition
- **GIVEN** Every element satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The iterable is checked
- **THEN** The result is true

#### Scenario: AnElementFailsCondition
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** At least one element does not satisfy the condition
- **WHEN** The iterable is checked
- **THEN** The result is false

### Sub-component: CurrentDateTime

> Implements: `datetime.now`

Obtains the current date and time for time-sensitive arena processing.

#### Requirement: GetCurrentDateTime

The system SHALL the block shall provide the current date and time.

#### Scenario: CurrentDateTimeIsReturned
- **GIVEN** A caller requires the current date and time
- **WHEN** The block is called
- **THEN** A current date and time value is returned

### Sub-component: CurrentDate

> Implements: `now.date`

Extracts the calendar date from the current date and time.

#### Requirement: ExtractDate

The system SHALL the block shall return the calendar date represented by the supplied date and time.

#### Scenario: DateIsExtracted
- **GIVEN** A date and time value is available
- **WHEN** Its date is requested
- **THEN** The corresponding calendar date is returned

### Sub-component: CurrentWeekday

> Implements: `now.weekday`

Determines the weekday of the current date.

#### Requirement: GetCurrentWeekday

The system SHALL the block shall return the weekday associated with the current date.

#### Scenario: WeekdayIsReturned
- **GIVEN** A current date and time value is available
- **WHEN** Its weekday is requested
- **THEN** The weekday value for its date is returned

### Sub-component: ModelLookup

> Implements: `model.get`

Retrieves a value from the arena state model by key.

#### Requirement: RetrieveModelValue

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve the value associated with the requested model key.

#### Scenario: ExistingKeyIsRetrieved
- **GIVEN** The model contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The key is looked up
- **THEN** Its associated value is returned

#### Scenario: MissingKeyUsesDefault
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The model does not contain the requested key
- **WHEN** The key is looked up with a default
- **THEN** The default value is returned

### Sub-component: ParseDateTime

> Implements: `datetime.fromisoformat`

Converts an ISO-formatted date and time string into a date-time value.

#### Requirement: ParseIsoDateTime

The system SHALL the block shall convert a valid iso-formatted string into a date-time value.

#### Scenario: ValidIsoDateTimeIsParsed
- **GIVEN** A valid ISO-formatted date and time string is supplied
- **WHEN** The string is parsed
- **THEN** The matching date-time value is returned

### Sub-component: DayWeekday

> Implements: `day.weekday`

Determines the weekday of a parsed day value.

#### Requirement: GetDayWeekday

The system SHALL the block shall return the weekday associated with the supplied day.

#### Scenario: WeekdayForDayIsReturned
- **GIVEN** A parsed day value is available
- **WHEN** Its weekday is requested
- **THEN** The weekday corresponding to that day is returned

### Sub-component: SplitEntry

> Implements: `entry.split`

Separates an entry into component fields.

The system SHALL the block shall divide an entry at the specified separator.

- **WHEN** The entry is split
- **THEN** The components on either side are returned as fields

### Sub-component: ClosedWeekParticipationReward

#### Scenario: OriginalTierIsPaidOnce

- **WHEN** The weekly reward settlement behavior is checked
- **THEN** The original tier reward is paid exactly once

- **WHEN** The player requests an encounter

> `source/server/exvius_events.py`

### Requirement: ValidatePolicy

The system SHALL the service shall validate an event policy and reject invalid policy data.

- **Implementation**: `validate_policy::source/server/exvius_events.py`

#### Scenario: ValidPolicy
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A policy containing valid values
- **WHEN** The policy is validated
- **THEN** Validation succeeds

#### Scenario: InvalidPolicy
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A policy containing invalid values
- **WHEN** The policy is validated
- **THEN** Validation raises a value error

### Requirement: SavePolicy

The system SHALL the service shall save a validated event policy and preserve the previous file contents in a versioned backup when replacing an existing policy.

- **Implementation**: `save_policy::source/server/exvius_events.py`

#### Scenario: SaveNewPolicy
- **GIVEN** A valid policy and no existing policy file
- **WHEN** The policy is saved
- **THEN** The policy is written to the policy path

#### Scenario: ReplaceExistingPolicy
- **GIVEN** A valid policy and an existing policy file
- **WHEN** The policy is saved
- **THEN** The new policy replaces the existing file and the previous bytes are preserved in a versioned backup

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidPolicy
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An invalid policy
- **WHEN** The policy is saved
- **THEN** Validation fails and the policy is not saved

### Requirement: ResolveEventFacts

The system SHALL the service shall resolve event facts by following the upstream first-in-file area, dungeon, and mission lookup order without guessing identifiers.

- **Implementation**: `event_facts::source/server/exvius_events.py`

#### Scenario: ResolveFirstMatchingEventFacts
- **GIVEN** Areas, dungeons, and missions contain matching event data
- **WHEN** Event facts are resolved
- **THEN** The first matching area-to-dungeon-to-mission facts are returned

#### Scenario: NoMatchingEventFacts
- **GIVEN** The supplied data has no matching event path
- **WHEN** Event facts are resolved
- **THEN** No identifiers are guessed

### Requirement: ApplyAvailability

The system SHALL the service shall extend availability only for gate rows that are both selected by policy and resolved, including rows already present in the specification.

- **Implementation**: `apply_availability::source/server/exvius_events.py`

#### Scenario: SelectedAndResolvedGate
- **GIVEN** A gate row is selected by policy and its area is resolved
- **WHEN** Availability is applied
- **THEN** The gate row's availability is extended

#### Scenario: UnselectedOrUnresolvedGate
- **GIVEN** A gate row is not selected or its area is unresolved
- **WHEN** Availability is applied
- **THEN** The gate row's availability is not extended

#### Scenario: ExistingGateRow
- **GIVEN** A selected and resolved gate row already exists in the specification
- **WHEN** Availability is applied
- **THEN** The existing row is included in the availability update

### Requirement: FormatNativeGateDates

The system SHALL the service shall render captured gate instants in the date-text format expected by gl9 while preserving those instants.

- **Implementation**: `native_gate_dates::source/server/exvius_events.py`

#### Scenario: PreserveCapturedInstants
- **GIVEN** A gate specification contains captured date instants
- **WHEN** Native gate dates are produced
- **THEN** The dates are spelled for GL9's reader without changing the represented instants

<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->

Resolves the season index associated with an area.

The system SHALL the function must return the index for a recognized area and must reject an unrecognized area.

#### Scenario: RecognizedArea
- **GIVEN** The area is configured in the season index
- **WHEN** season_index is called
- **THEN** Its season index is returned.

#### Scenario: UnrecognizedArea
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The area is not configured
- **WHEN** season_index is called
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised.

### Sub-component: Policy

> Implements: `policy`

Loads and provides the Fortuna policy configuration.

#### Requirement: LoadPolicy

The system SHALL the function must provide the policy configuration used by fortuna operations.

#### Scenario: PolicyRequested
- **GIVEN** Fortuna policy configuration is available
- **WHEN** policy is called
- **THEN** The policy configuration is returned.

### Sub-component: FeatureSetdefault

> Implements: `feature.setdefault`

Ensures a feature mapping contains a value for a specified key.

#### Requirement: EnsureFeatureValue

The system SHALL the mapping operation must return the existing value for a key or set and return the supplied default when the key is absent.

#### Scenario: KeyAbsent
- **GIVEN** The feature mapping does not contain the key
- **WHEN** setdefault is called with a default
- **THEN** The key is assigned the default and that value is returned.

#### Scenario: KeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The feature mapping contains the key
- **WHEN** setdefault is called
- **THEN** The existing value is returned without replacement.

### Sub-component: GroupsSetdefault

> Implements: `groups.setdefault`

#### Scenario: GroupKeyAbsent
- **GIVEN** The groups mapping does not contain the key
- **WHEN** setdefault is called with a default
- **THEN** The key is assigned the default and that value is returned.

#### Scenario: GroupKeyPresent
- **GIVEN** The groups mapping contains the key
- **WHEN** setdefault is called
- **THEN** The existing value is returned without replacement.

### Sub-component: TestAllClashLevelRewardsResolveAndOnlyPayNewLevels

> Implements: `test_all_clash_level_rewards_resolve_and_only_pay_new_levels`

Verifies clash level rewards resolve correctly and are awarded only for newly reached levels.

#### Requirement: VerifyLevelRewardResolutionAndAwarding

The system SHALL the test must verify that configured clash level rewards resolve and that only newly reached reward levels are paid.

#### Scenario: RewardsResolveAndOnlyNewLevelsPay
- **GIVEN** A player has reached levels that include both previously claimed and newly available rewards
- **WHEN** the clash level reward behavior is evaluated
- **THEN** All configured rewards resolve and only newly available levels are awarded.

### Sub-component: TestClashRewardPagesAndExplicitCollectionPersistOnce

> Implements: `test_clash_reward_pages_and_explicit_collection_persist_once`

Verifies clash reward pages and explicit reward collection persist without duplicate payment.

#### Requirement: VerifyRewardCollectionPersistence

The system SHALL the test must verify that explicit clash reward collection persists and does not pay the same reward more than once.

#### Scenario: CollectOnceAcrossRestore
- **GIVEN** A player collects a clash reward and their state is then restored
- **WHEN** the reward is collected again
- **THEN** The reward is not paid twice and the restored inventory reflects the persisted collection.
