> `source/server/exvius_gacha_select.py`

### Requirement: SelectUnit

The system SHALL the service shall validate the selected banner and available unit reward, spend the required select tickets, and grant the unit. invalid requests or insufficient tickets shall be declined without changing player state.

- **Implementation**: `select_unit::source/server/exvius_gacha_select.py`

#### Scenario: GrantEligibleSelectedUnit
- **GIVEN** An active player, a valid selection banner and detail, an available unit reward, and sufficient
tickets
- **WHEN** select_unit is called with the selection request
- **THEN** The required tickets are spent and the selected unit is granted in the refreshed response

#### Scenario: DeclineInvalidOrUnaffordableUnitSelection
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player is absent or frozen, the banner or reward is invalid or unavailable, the reward cannot be
credited, or tickets are insufficient
- **WHEN** select_unit is called
- **THEN** The request returns a decline response without granting the unit or spending tickets

#### Scenario: RestorePlayerStateIfUnitGrantFails
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A valid selection request whose unit grant raises an exception
- **WHEN** select_unit attempts to grant the unit
- **THEN** The player's data and dirty state are restored before the exception is raised

### Requirement: SelectPrism

The system SHALL the service shall validate the selected prism reward, spend the required select tickets, and grant the prism. invalid requests or insufficient tickets shall be declined without changing player state.

- **Implementation**: `select_prism::source/server/exvius_gacha_select.py`

#### Scenario: GrantEligibleSelectedPrism
- **GIVEN** An active player, a valid selection banner and detail, an available prism reward, and sufficient
tickets
- **WHEN** select_prism is called with the selection request
- **THEN** The required tickets are spent and the selected prism is granted in the refreshed response

#### Scenario: DeclineInvalidOrUnaffordablePrismSelection
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player is absent or frozen, the banner or reward is invalid or unavailable, the reward cannot be
credited, or tickets are insufficient
- **WHEN** select_prism is called
- **THEN** The request returns a decline response without granting the prism or spending tickets

#### Scenario: RestorePlayerStateIfPrismGrantFails
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A valid selection request whose prism grant fails
- **WHEN** select_prism attempts to grant the prism
- **THEN** The player's data and dirty state are restored before the exception is raised

> `source/server/exvius_offline.py`

### Requirement: ProfileEnvironment

The system SHALL the service shall validate profile choices and produce environment switches for banner selection, campaign selection, standard banners, and explicit event areas. invalid or incomplete choices shall raise a validation error.

- **Implementation**: `profile_environment::source/server/exvius_offline.py`

#### Scenario: ConfigureAllBanners
- **GIVEN** A profile selecting all banners and containing explicit numeric event area IDs
- **WHEN** profile_environment is called
- **THEN** The returned environment enables all banner and campaign choices and includes the event area IDs and
gate window

#### Scenario: ConfigureSelectedBannerIDs
- **GIVEN** A profile selecting IDs with at least one numeric banner ID and explicit numeric event area IDs
- **WHEN** profile_environment is called
- **THEN** The returned environment includes the selected IDs and campaign mode all

#### Scenario: ConfigureACampaign
- **GIVEN** A profile selecting a valid campaign key and containing explicit numeric event area IDs
- **WHEN** profile_environment is called
- **THEN** The returned environment includes the campaign key and event area configuration

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidBannerOrCampaignSelection
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The profile has an unsupported banner mode, empty or nonnumeric IDs, or an invalid campaign key
- **WHEN** profile_environment is called
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised describing the invalid selection

#### Scenario: RequireExplicitEventAreas
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The profile has no event area IDs or contains a nonnumeric event area ID
- **WHEN** profile_environment is called
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised requiring explicit event area IDs

> `source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

### Requirement: ValidateRaidConfig

The system SHALL the service shall validate raid configuration structure, identifiers, and thresholds before it is used.

- **Implementation**: `validate_config::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: ValidConfiguration
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A configuration with valid event, mission, and milestone definitions
- **WHEN** the configuration is validated
- **THEN** Validation succeeds.

#### Scenario: InvalidOrUnsupportedMilestone
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A configuration containing invalid identifiers, thresholds, or unsupported personal milestones
- **WHEN** the configuration is validated
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised.

### Requirement: RecordCompletion

The system SHALL the service shall record an accepted mission completion for configured shared raid progress, respecting completion identity and success status.

- **Implementation**: `record_completion::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: SuccessfulConfiguredCompletion
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A valid configured mission and a successful completion
- **WHEN** the completion is recorded
- **THEN** Shared raid progress is updated for that completion.

#### Scenario: UnsuccessfulOrInvalidCompletion
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An unsuccessful completion or a mission not accepted by the configuration
- **WHEN** the completion is recorded
- **THEN** Progress is not credited and invalid input is rejected.

### Requirement: ViewProgress

The system SHALL the service shall present raid progress using locally reachable milestone thresholds derived from original thresholds.

- **Implementation**: `progress_view::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: ProgressViewWithMilestones
- **GIVEN** An event definition and saved progress
- **WHEN** the progress view is requested
- **THEN** It presents progress and locally adjusted milestone thresholds.

### Requirement: ClaimSharedMilestone

The system SHALL the service shall record an explicit shared milestone claim and return the original reward content without crediting inventory.

- **Implementation**: `claim_shared_milestone::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: ClaimAvailableMilestone
- **GIVEN** A valid event with an achieved, unclaimed milestone
- **WHEN** the milestone is claimed
- **THEN** The claim is recorded and the original reward content is returned without inventory changes.

#### Scenario: ClaimUnavailableMilestone
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An unknown, unachieved, or already claimed milestone
- **WHEN** the milestone is claimed
- **THEN** The claim is rejected.

### Requirement: SaveState

The system SHALL the service shall persist standalone raid state atomically to the requested path.

#### Scenario: SaveStandaloneState
- **GIVEN** Raid state and a writable destination
- **WHEN** the state is saved
- **THEN** The state is written atomically.

### Requirement: CreditConfiguredRaid

The system SHALL the service shall apply configured raid progress at the runtime seam after an accepted mission completion, only when an explicit mapping exists.

- **Implementation**: `credit_configured_raid::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: MappedCompletion
- **GIVEN** An accepted completion with an explicit raid mapping
- **WHEN** configured raid credit is applied
- **THEN** The mapped raid progress is updated.

#### Scenario: NoExplicitMapping
- **GIVEN** An accepted completion without a configured mapping
- **WHEN** configured raid credit is applied
- **THEN** No raid progress is credited.

### Requirement: LoadPersonalEvent

The system SHALL the service shall resolve supported personal raid event data by mission or dungeon, rejecting events whose original reward table is unavailable.

- **Implementation**: `personal_event::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: SupportedPersonalEvent
- **GIVEN** A mission or dungeon associated with recovered event and reward data
- **WHEN** the personal event is requested
- **THEN** Its event definition is returned.

#### Scenario: UnsupportedPersonalEvent
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An unknown event or one without recovered original rewards
- **WHEN** the personal event is requested
- **THEN** A ValueError is raised.

### Requirement: ObservePersonalRaid

The system SHALL the service shall record a player's personal raid encounter using the actual encounter hit points.

- **Implementation**: `observe_personal_raid::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: ObserveEncounter
- **GIVEN** A supported personal raid mission and its actual encounter specification
- **WHEN** the raid is observed
- **THEN** The encounter state retains its actual HP for scoring.

### Requirement: RecordPartyAction

The system SHALL the service shall record distinct native party actors performing the required skill sequence for a secret unlock.

- **Implementation**: `record_party_action::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: DistinctPartyActions
- **GIVEN** Party members perform qualifying skills
- **WHEN** each party action is recorded
- **THEN** Distinct native actors are tracked for the secret sequence.

### Requirement: CompleteSecretUnlock

The system SHALL the service shall complete the personal raid secret unlock for a mission when its unlock requirements are met.

- **Implementation**: `complete_secret_unlock::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: RequirementsMet
- **GIVEN** The player's recorded actions satisfy the mission's secret sequence
- **WHEN** the secret unlock is completed
- **THEN** The unlock is recorded.

#### Scenario: RequirementsUnmet
- **GIVEN** The player's actions do not satisfy the secret sequence
- **WHEN** the secret unlock is completed
- **THEN** The unlock is not granted.

### Requirement: BuildPersonalMenuGroups

The system SHALL the service shall provide personal raid menu groups for the player's event state.

- **Implementation**: `personal_menu_groups::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: BuildMenuGroups
- **GIVEN** A player and personal raid event
- **WHEN** menu groups are requested
- **THEN** Groups reflect the player's personal raid state.

### Requirement: BuildPersonalRaidMenu

The system SHALL the service shall provide the personal raid menu response for a raid specification and request.

- **Implementation**: `personal_raid_menu::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: BuildRaidMenu
- **GIVEN** A raid specification and menu request
- **WHEN** the personal raid menu is requested
- **THEN** A menu response for the raid is returned.

### Requirement: ApplyPersonalRaidResult

The system SHALL the service shall credit reported damage once per accepted personal raid attempt and deliver mail for crossed milestones, rolling back partial rewards if mail delivery fails.

- **Implementation**: `apply_personal_raid_result::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: AcceptedAttemptCrossesMilestones
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A valid accepted result reporting damage that crosses personal milestones
- **WHEN** the raid result is applied
- **THEN** Damage is credited once and crossed milestone rewards are mailed.

#### Scenario: DuplicateOrInvalidAttempt
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A result that is duplicate or fails acceptance checks
- **WHEN** the raid result is applied
- **THEN** Damage and rewards are not credited again.

#### Scenario: MailDeliveryFailure
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An accepted result crosses milestones but mail delivery fails
- **WHEN** the raid result is applied
- **THEN** Partial mail, coins, and points are rolled back.

### Requirement: BuildPersonalRewardsPage

The system SHALL the service shall provide content for the original raid screen's embedded personal-reward panel.

- **Implementation**: `personal_rewards_page::source/server/exvius_raid_progress.py`

#### Scenario: RewardsPageForSupportedDungeon
- **GIVEN** A player and supported personal raid dungeon
- **WHEN** the personal rewards page is requested
- **THEN** The embedded panel content reflects the player's personal rewards.

> `source/server/exvius_repeat.py`

### Requirement: BeginAttempt

The system SHALL the service shall begin an attempt for a player and mission, preserving any supplied battle captures for later reward settlement.

- **Implementation**: `begin_attempt::source/server/exvius_repeat.py`

#### Scenario: StartAMissionAttempt
- **GIVEN** A player and mission ID, with optional captures
- **WHEN** An attempt is begun
- **THEN** The service records attempt state for subsequent completion and reward claims

### Requirement: ParseRewards

The system SHALL the service shall parse a raw reward encoding into reward entries and reject malformed or unsupported entries.

- **Implementation**: `parse_rewards::source/server/exvius_repeat.py`

#### Scenario: ParseValidRewardEncoding
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A supported reward string
- **WHEN** The string is parsed
- **THEN** The service returns the represented reward entries

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidRewardEncoding
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A malformed or unsupported reward string
- **WHEN** The string is parsed
- **THEN** The service rejects the invalid input

### Requirement: CompleteAttempt

The system SHALL the service shall settle a completed attempt as one serialized reward transaction, applying eligible mission and challenge rewards and recording completion outcomes.

- **Implementation**: `complete_attempt::source/server/exvius_repeat.py`

#### Scenario: SettleAClearedAttempt
- **GIVEN** An active attempt with completion and challenge results
- **WHEN** The attempt is completed
- **THEN** Eligible rewards and completion records are applied in one transaction

#### Scenario: DoNotCreditBlockedRewards
- **GIVEN** A reward the player cannot receive under the supplied checks
- **WHEN** The attempt is completed
- **THEN** The blocked reward is not granted

#### Scenario: DoNotDuplicateChallengeRewards
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A challenge reward has already been credited for the attempt
- **WHEN** The attempt is completed again
- **THEN** The reward is not credited twice

### Requirement: ClaimMissionRewards

The system SHALL the service shall claim normal mission loot at most once for a loaded attempt.

- **Implementation**: `claim_mission_rewards::source/server/exvius_repeat.py`

#### Scenario: ClaimMissionLootOnce
- **GIVEN** A loaded attempt with unclaimed mission loot
- **WHEN** Mission rewards are claimed
- **THEN** The normal loot is credited and the attempt is marked as claimed

#### Scenario: RepeatAMissionLootClaim
- **GIVEN** Mission loot for the loaded attempt has already been claimed
- **WHEN** Mission rewards are claimed again
- **THEN** The loot is not credited again

### Requirement: ClaimQuestProgress

The system SHALL the service shall use a durable completion receipt at most once to update quest progress counters.

- **Implementation**: `claim_quest_progress::source/server/exvius_repeat.py`

#### Scenario: ApplyQuestProgressFromReceipt
- **GIVEN** A durable completion receipt exists and has not been consumed
- **WHEN** Quest progress is claimed
- **THEN** The corresponding quest counters are updated once

#### Scenario: AvoidDuplicateQuestProgress
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The completion receipt has already been consumed
- **WHEN** Quest progress is claimed again
- **THEN** Quest counters are not incremented again

### Requirement: ObserveLoad

The system SHALL the service shall observe a mission load and identify the applicable repeat-attempt state for the supplied mission and battle-bundle status.

- **Implementation**: `observe_load::source/server/exvius_repeat.py`

#### Scenario: ObserveAMissionLoad
- **GIVEN** A mission specification, mission ID, and battle-bundle indicator
- **WHEN** The load is observed
- **THEN** The service updates or returns the corresponding attempt observation

### Requirement: RepeatChallengeRewards

The system SHALL the service shall process repeat challenge rewards from the supplied mission specification and result payload, applying the configured completion and reward projections.

- **Implementation**: `repeat_challenge_rewards::source/server/exvius_repeat.py`

#### Scenario: ProcessRepeatChallengeResults
- **GIVEN** A mission specification and plaintext result containing challenge outcomes
- **WHEN** Repeat challenge rewards are processed
- **THEN** Applicable challenge and related completion rewards are settled

> `source/server/exvius_rewards.py`

### Requirement: Parse

The system SHALL the service shall parse a raw reward descriptor into structured reward data, preserving reward-specific attributes such as unit level and trust target, and shall reject malformed descriptors.

- **Implementation**: `parse::source/server/exvius_rewards.py`

#### Scenario: ValidRatedUnitReward
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A reward descriptor for a rated unit with level and trust attributes
- **WHEN** parse is called with the descriptor
- **THEN** The returned reward data preserves the unit, level, and trust values

#### Scenario: MalformedRewardDescriptor
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A reward descriptor contains invalid fields
- **WHEN** parse is called with the descriptor
- **THEN** The service raises ValueError


## Sub-components

> `ExviusRewards` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: All

> Implements: `all`

Determines whether every item in a collection satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Requirement: EvaluateAllItems

The system SHALL the block shall report whether every item in the input collection satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: AllItemsSatisfyCondition
- **GIVEN** A collection whose items all satisfy the condition
- **WHEN** The block evaluates the collection
- **THEN** It SHALL report that every item satisfies the condition

#### Scenario: AnItemDoesNotSatisfyCondition
- **GIVEN** A collection containing an item that does not satisfy the condition
- **WHEN** The block evaluates the collection
- **THEN** It SHALL report that not every item satisfies the condition

### Sub-component: ValueError

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents an error raised when a supplied value is invalid.

#### Requirement: SignalInvalidValue

The system SHALL the block shall provide an error outcome when a value cannot be accepted.

#### Scenario: InvalidValueIsRejected
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A value that violates an expected constraint
- **WHEN** The block signals the invalid value
- **THEN** It SHALL produce a value error

### Sub-component: UnitMst

> Implements: `unit_mst`

Provides unit master data used to associate rewards with units.

#### Requirement: ResolveUnitRewardTarget

The system SHALL the block shall preserve reward trust targets and associate unit-bound rewards with their matching unit.

#### Scenario: EventMooglePreservesSpecificTrustTarget
- **GIVEN** An event moogle reward with a specific trust target
- **WHEN** The reward is resolved against unit master data
- **THEN** The specific trust target SHALL be preserved

#### Scenario: ExRewardsMapGlobalTarget
- **GIVEN** EX-specific STMR moogles and a global target mapping
- **WHEN** The rewards are resolved
- **THEN** The global target mapping SHALL be applied to the corresponding rewards

#### Scenario: Ex2MoogleMatchesUnit
- **GIVEN** An EX2-bound moogle and multiple possible units
- **WHEN** The reward is resolved
- **THEN** The STMR reward SHALL be granted only to its matching unit

### Sub-component: FieldStrip

> Implements: `field.strip`

Removes surrounding whitespace from a descriptor field.

#### Requirement: NormalizeFieldWhitespace

The system SHALL the block shall remove leading and trailing whitespace from the field before it is processed.

#### Scenario: FieldHasSurroundingWhitespace
- **GIVEN** A field with leading and trailing whitespace
- **WHEN** The block normalizes the field
- **THEN** It SHALL return the field without surrounding whitespace

### Sub-component: EntrySplit

> Implements: `entry.split`

Separates an entry into parts using the requested delimiter.

#### Requirement: SeparateEntryParts

The system SHALL the block shall separate an entry into parts at occurrences of the specified delimiter.

#### Scenario: EntryContainsDelimiter
- **GIVEN** An entry containing the specified delimiter
- **WHEN** The block separates the entry
- **THEN** It SHALL return the parts divided at the delimiter

### Sub-component: FIsascii

> Implements: `f.isascii`

Checks whether a field contains only ASCII characters.

#### Requirement: CheckAsciiField

The system SHALL the block shall indicate whether the field contains only ascii characters.

#### Scenario: FieldContainsNonAsciiCharacter
- **GIVEN** A field containing a non-ASCII character
- **WHEN** The block checks the field
- **THEN** It SHALL indicate that the field is not ASCII-only

#### Scenario: FieldContainsOnlyAsciiCharacters
- **GIVEN** A field containing only ASCII characters
- **WHEN** The block checks the field
- **THEN** It SHALL indicate that the field is ASCII-only

### Sub-component: FIsdigit

> Implements: `f.isdigit`

Checks whether a field consists of digit characters.

#### Requirement: CheckDigitField

The system SHALL the block shall indicate whether the field consists of digit characters.

#### Scenario: FieldContainsDigits
- **GIVEN** A non-empty field made up of digit characters
- **WHEN** The block checks the field
- **THEN** It SHALL indicate that the field consists of digits

#### Scenario: FieldContainsNonDigit
- **GIVEN** A field containing a non-digit character
- **WHEN** The block checks the field
- **THEN** It SHALL indicate that the field does not consist solely of digits

### Sub-component: DescriptorSplit

> Implements: `descriptor.split`

Separates a reward descriptor into components using the requested delimiter.

#### Requirement: SeparateDescriptorComponents

The system SHALL the block shall separate the descriptor into components at occurrences of the specified delimiter.

#### Scenario: DescriptorContainsDelimiter
- **GIVEN** A descriptor containing the specified delimiter
- **WHEN** The block separates the descriptor
- **THEN** It SHALL return the components divided at the delimiter

### Sub-component: ResultAppend

> Implements: `result.append`

Adds a parsed reward value to the accumulated result.

#### Requirement: AccumulateParsedReward

The system SHALL the block shall add the parsed reward value to the end of the result collection.

#### Scenario: ParsedRewardIsAdded
- **GIVEN** An accumulated result and a parsed reward value
- **WHEN** The block adds the reward value
- **THEN** The value SHALL appear at the end of the result collection

### Sub-component: TestRatedUnitRewardsPreserveLevelAndTrust

> Implements: `test_rated_unit_rewards_preserve_level_and_trust`

Verifies that parsing rated unit rewards retains their level and trust values and rejects invalid descriptors.

#### Requirement: PreserveRatedRewardAttributes

The system SHALL the block shall verify that parsing a rated unit reward preserves its level and trust attributes.

#### Scenario: RatedRewardAttributesArePreserved
- **GIVEN** A valid rated unit reward descriptor with level and trust values
- **WHEN** The descriptor is parsed and checked
- **THEN** The parsed reward SHALL retain the specified level and trust

#### Scenario: InvalidRatedRewardRaisesError
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** An invalid rated unit reward descriptor
- **WHEN** The descriptor is parsed
- **THEN** Parsing SHALL raise the expected error

### Sub-component: TestAllRestoredSeriesPointChallengeRowsPayOnce

> Implements: `test_all_restored_series_point_challenge_rows_pay_once`

Verifies that restored series point challenge rows award their rewards at most once.

#### Requirement: VerifyChallengeAwardsPayOnce

The system SHALL the block shall verify that each restored series point challenge row pays its award only once across an attempt.

#### Scenario: ChallengeRowAwardsOnce
- **GIVEN** Restored series point challenge rows and an attempt that completes a qualifying mission
- **WHEN** The attempt is completed and awards are evaluated
- **THEN** Each qualifying row SHALL pay once

#### Scenario: RepeatedCompletionDoesNotPayAgain
- **GIVEN** A challenge row that has already paid for the attempt
- **WHEN** The same qualifying completion is processed again
- **THEN** The row SHALL NOT pay a second time

> `source/server/exvius_shop_catalogue.py`

### Requirement: LoadCatalogue

The system SHALL the service shall assemble shop details and products from configured catalogue data and return the resulting catalogue.

#### Scenario: CatalogueIsAvailable
- **GIVEN** Catalogue data is configured
- **WHEN** the catalogue is requested
- **THEN** the service returns shop details and products derived from that data

#### Scenario: CatalogueDataIsCached
- **GIVEN** the catalogue has already been loaded
- **WHEN** the catalogue is requested again
- **THEN** the service reuses the cached catalogue

### Requirement: GenerateReceiptKey

The system SHALL the service shall generate a receipt key for a product and offer.

- **Implementation**: `receipt_key::source/server/exvius_shop_catalogue.py`

#### Scenario: ReceiptKeyForAnOffer
- **GIVEN** A product identifier and offer identifier
- **WHEN** a receipt key is requested
- **THEN** the service returns a key associated with that product and offer

### Requirement: GetLabels

The system SHALL the service shall provide labels used to describe shop catalogue entries.

- **Implementation**: `labels::source/server/exvius_shop_catalogue.py`

#### Scenario: LabelsAreAvailable
- **GIVEN** Shop label data is available
- **WHEN** labels are requested
- **THEN** the service returns the catalogue labels

### Requirement: GetEntry

The system SHALL the service shall resolve a catalogue entry for the supplied specification and request.

#### Scenario: MatchingEntryIsRequested
- **GIVEN** A specification and request describing an entry
- **WHEN** the entry is resolved
- **THEN** the service returns the corresponding catalogue entry

### Requirement: PurchaseProduct

The system SHALL the service shall validate a purchase request, enforce applicable purchase constraints, process payment, grant rewards, record the purchase, and return a response.

- **Implementation**: `purchase::source/server/exvius_shop_catalogue.py`

#### Scenario: ValidPurchase
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A valid product request with sufficient payment and remaining purchase allowance
- **WHEN** the purchase is processed
- **THEN** payment is collected, rewards are granted, the purchase is recorded, and a response is returned

#### Scenario: PurchaseRequestIsInvalid
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A request that fails purchase validation
- **WHEN** the purchase is processed
- **THEN** the service rejects the request without granting the purchase rewards


## Sub-components

> `ShopCatalogueService` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: LruCache

- **WHEN** The function is called again with the same arguments
- **THEN** The previously computed result is returned

### Sub-component: Catalogue

> Implements: `catalogue`

Builds and returns the catalogue from source rows, localized names, currencies, and shop data.

#### Requirement: AssembleCatalogue

The system SHALL the operation shall combine catalogue rows and supporting data into catalogue details and products.

#### Scenario: BuildCatalogueFromAvailableData
- **GIVEN** Catalogue rows and supporting data are available
- **WHEN** The catalogue is requested
- **THEN** A catalogue containing the available details and products is returned

#### Requirement: ReuseCachedCatalogue

The system SHALL the operation should reuse a previously built catalogue when the cached result is available.

#### Scenario: RepeatedCatalogueRequest
- **GIVEN** The catalogue has already been built
- **WHEN** The catalogue is requested again
- **THEN** The previously built catalogue is returned

### Sub-component: Require

> Implements: `_require`

Enforces the presence of required input data.

#### Requirement: ValidateRequiredValue

The system SHALL the operation shall accept a present required value and shall reject a missing required value.

#### Scenario: RejectMissingRequiredValue
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A required value is absent
- **WHEN** The requirement is checked
- **THEN** A validation error is raised

### Sub-component: Int

> Implements: `_int`

Converts or validates a value as an integer for shop operations.

#### Requirement: ValidateIntegerValue

The system SHALL the operation shall provide an integer for valid input and shall reject input that cannot be interpreted as an integer.

#### Scenario: ConvertValidIntegerInput
- **GIVEN** An input represents an integer
- **WHEN** The integer value is requested
- **THEN** The corresponding integer is returned

### Sub-component: Feature

> Implements: `_feature`

Determines whether a requested shop feature is enabled.

#### Requirement: CheckFeatureAvailability

The system SHALL the operation shall report whether the requested feature is enabled.

#### Scenario: ReportEnabledFeature
- **GIVEN** The requested feature is enabled
- **WHEN** Its availability is checked
- **THEN** The operation reports that it is enabled

### Sub-component: ReceiptKey

> Implements: `receipt_key`

Creates a receipt identifier for a product offer using the current local time.

#### Requirement: CreateReceiptIdentifier

The system SHALL the operation shall return a receipt key associated with the supplied product and offer.

#### Scenario: CreateKeyForProductOffer
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A product identifier and offer are supplied
- **WHEN** A receipt key is requested
- **THEN** A receipt key for that product offer is returned

### Sub-component: Rewards

> Implements: `_rewards`

Determines the rewards associated with a shop operation.

#### Requirement: ResolveRewards

The system SHALL the operation shall provide the rewards associated with the supplied shop data.

#### Scenario: ResolveConfiguredRewards
- **GIVEN** Shop data specifies rewards
- **WHEN** Rewards are resolved
- **THEN** The specified rewards are provided

### Sub-component: Pay

> Implements: `_pay`

Processes payment for a shop purchase.

#### Requirement: ProcessPurchasePayment

The system SHALL the operation shall process payment for the requested purchase and report its outcome.

#### Scenario: ProcessEligiblePurchase
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A purchase request contains valid payment details
- **WHEN** Payment is processed
- **THEN** The payment outcome is returned

### Sub-component: Grant

> Implements: `_grant`

Grants purchase rewards to the appropriate recipient.

#### Requirement: GrantPurchaseRewards

The system SHALL the operation shall grant the rewards associated with a completed purchase.

#### Scenario: GrantRewardsAfterPurchase
- **GIVEN** A purchase has completed and rewards are available
- **WHEN** The rewards are granted
- **THEN** The recipient receives the specified rewards

### Sub-component: Store

> Implements: `_store`

Persists shop purchase or receipt data.

#### Requirement: PersistShopData

The system SHALL the operation shall store the supplied shop data for subsequent access.

#### Scenario: StorePurchaseData
- **GIVEN** Shop purchase data is available
- **WHEN** The data is stored
- **THEN** A storage outcome is returned

### Sub-component: Reply

> Implements: `_reply`

Formats and returns a response for a shop operation.

#### Requirement: BuildShopReply

The system SHALL the operation shall produce a reply representing the shop operation outcome.

#### Scenario: ReplyWithOperationOutcome
- **GIVEN** A shop operation outcome is available
- **WHEN** A reply is created
- **THEN** A response representing the outcome is returned

### Sub-component: NodeGet

> Implements: `node.get`

Retrieves a value from a catalogue node with optional default behavior.

#### Requirement: RetrieveNodeValue

The system SHALL the operation shall return the value associated with a node key or its supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: RetrievePresentNodeValue
- **GIVEN** A node contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: CountsGet

> Implements: `counts.get`

Retrieves a count by key with optional default behavior.

#### Requirement: RetrieveCount

The system SHALL the operation shall return the count associated with a key or the supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: RetrieveConfiguredCount
- **GIVEN** The counts mapping contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The count is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated count is returned

> `source/server/ffbe_banner.py`

### Requirement: SelectBannerCampaign

The system SHALL the service shall select the active banner campaign and return its label and rows.

#### Scenario: ActiveCampaignSelection
- **GIVEN** A banner campaign configuration is available
- **WHEN** banner_selection is called
- **THEN** The active campaign label and its rows are returned

### Requirement: GetMarqueeRows

The system SHALL the service shall return wire-format marquee rows for the active selection and return an empty list when the feature is off.

#### Scenario: FeatureEnabled
- **GIVEN** The home marquee feature is enabled and an active selection exists
- **WHEN** marquee_rows is called
- **THEN** Wire-format rows for the active selection are returned

#### Scenario: FeatureDisabled
- **GIVEN** The home marquee feature is off
- **WHEN** marquee_rows is called
- **THEN** An empty list is returned

### Requirement: ServeHomeMarquee

The system SHALL the service shall populate the marquee group in eligible replies and enable the home marquee feature flag.

#### Scenario: ReplyCarriesMarqueeGroup
- **GIVEN** A reply specification carries the marquee group
- **WHEN** serve_home_marquee is called
- **THEN** The marquee group is populated and the feature flag is turned on

#### Scenario: ReplyDoesNotCarryMarqueeGroup
- **GIVEN** A reply specification does not carry the marquee group
- **WHEN** serve_home_marquee is called
- **THEN** No marquee group is added


## Sub-components

- **THEN** It returns the campaign document.

- **THEN** It returns the handled note.

- **WHEN** The block lowercases the key
- **THEN** The result contains the key characters in lowercase.

- **THEN** It returns the value associated with that key.

### Sub-component: EraLookup

- **WHEN** The era lookup runs
- **THEN** It returns the value associated with that key.

### Sub-component: EntryLookup

> `source/server/ffbe_battle.py`

### Requirement: RollBattleDrops

The system SHALL the service shall roll each served drop list so its contents can vary between battles.

#### Scenario: RollServedDrops
- **GIVEN** A battle specification contains served drop lists
- **WHEN** drops are rolled for the battle
- **THEN** Each list is replaced with a roll of its contents

### Requirement: ApplyBattleResultDrops

The system SHALL the service shall put the current battle’s rolled drops into the result screen’s item channels.

#### Scenario: ShowCurrentBattleDrops
- **GIVEN** A battle has rolled drops
- **WHEN** result drops are applied
- **THEN** The result item channels contain that battle’s drops

### Sub-component: CounterInitialization

- **WHEN** The counter is initialized
- **THEN** The counter SHALL report zero for an unrecorded drop key

- **THEN** It SHALL report a match

> Implements: `ValueError`

Represents an invalid value encountered while interpreting mission-result data.

#### Requirement: SignalInvalidValue

The system SHALL the block shall signal that a supplied value is invalid for the expected operation.

#### Scenario: InvalidValueRejected
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Mission-result data contains a value that cannot be accepted
- **WHEN** The invalid value is handled
- **THEN** A ValueError SHALL be raised or recognized

### Sub-component: MissionRow

> Implements: `mission_row`

Loads or resolves the row associated with a mission.

#### Requirement: ResolveMissionRow

The system SHALL the function shall resolve the mission row required for result interpretation.

#### Scenario: NamedMissionRowResolved
- **GIVEN** Loaded mission data contains a row for the requested mission
- **WHEN** The mission row is resolved
- **THEN** The function SHALL return that row

### Sub-component: OperationLookup

> Implements: `operation.get`

Retrieves a field from a mission-result operation.

#### Requirement: LookupOperationField

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve the requested operation field or its default.

#### Scenario: OperationFieldAvailable
- **GIVEN** The operation contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The block retrieves it
- **THEN** It SHALL return the field value

### Sub-component: ResultOperationLookup

> Implements: `result_operation.get`

Retrieves a field from the result operation record.

#### Requirement: LookupResultOperationField

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve the requested result-operation field or its default.

#### Scenario: ResultOperationFieldAvailable
- **GIVEN** The result-operation record contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The block looks it up
- **THEN** It SHALL return the field value

### Sub-component: DigitValidation

> Implements: `helper.isdigit`

Checks whether a helper value contains only digits.

#### Requirement: ValidateDigitValue

The system SHALL the block shall report whether the supplied helper value consists only of digits.

#### Scenario: NumericHelperValue
- **GIVEN** The helper value consists only of decimal digits
- **WHEN** Digit validity is checked
- **THEN** The block SHALL report that the value is numeric

### Sub-component: MetadataLookup

> Implements: `meta.get`

Retrieves a field from mission-result metadata.

#### Requirement: LookupMetadataField

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve the requested metadata field or its default.

#### Scenario: MetadataFieldAvailable
- **GIVEN** Metadata contains the requested field
- **WHEN** The block looks it up
- **THEN** It SHALL return that field value

### Sub-component: EntryPartition

> Implements: `entry.partition`

Separates an entry into the text before, the separator, and the text after a delimiter.

#### Requirement: PartitionMissionEntry

The system SHALL the block shall partition an entry around the first occurrence of the specified separator.

#### Scenario: EntryContainsDelimiter
- **GIVEN** An entry contains the specified separator
- **WHEN** The entry is partitioned
- **THEN** The block SHALL return the text before and after the first separator

### Sub-component: RegexFullMatch

> Implements: `re.fullmatch`

Checks whether all of a value matches a specified pattern.

#### Requirement: MatchEntireValue

The system SHALL the block shall report a match only when the entire value satisfies the pattern.

#### Scenario: PartialValueRejected
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Only part of a value satisfies the pattern
- **WHEN** The entire value is checked
- **THEN** The block SHALL report no full match

### Sub-component: ElapsedDigitValidation

> Implements: `elapsed.isdigit`

Checks whether an elapsed-time value contains only digits.

#### Requirement: ValidateElapsedDigits

The system SHALL the block shall report whether the elapsed-time value consists only of digits.

#### Scenario: ElapsedDigitsAccepted
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The elapsed-time value contains only digits
- **WHEN** The value is checked
- **THEN** The block SHALL report that it is numeric

### Sub-component: ParameterDigitValidation

> Implements: `p.isdigit`

Checks whether a parameter value contains only digits.

#### Requirement: ValidateParameterDigits

The system SHALL the block shall report whether the parameter value consists only of digits.

#### Scenario: ParameterContainsNonDigit
- **GIVEN** The parameter value contains a non-digit character
- **WHEN** Digit validity is checked
- **THEN** The block SHALL report that it is not numeric

### Sub-component: StripRecordValue

Removes leading and trailing whitespace from a record value.

The system SHALL the block shall remove leading and trailing whitespace from the supplied value.

#### Scenario: RecordWhitespaceRemoved
- **GIVEN** A record value has surrounding whitespace
- **WHEN** The value is stripped
- **THEN** The returned value SHALL have no leading or trailing whitespace

### Sub-component: RowParsing

Splits a mission row into its component values.

The system SHALL the block shall divide the mission row at the specified separator.

- **WHEN** The row is split
- **THEN** The block SHALL return the fields as separate values

### Sub-component: MissionLookup

Retrieves a field from mission data.

- **WHEN** The block looks it up
- **THEN** It SHALL return the field value

### Sub-component: MissionResultContext

### Sub-component: ChallengeTable

### Sub-component: SwitchPosition

- **THEN** The returned input SHALL have no surrounding whitespace

Adds a recorded value to a set of values already received.

- **THEN** The test SHALL confirm that the switch updates remain present

> `source/server/ffbe_battle.py`

### Requirement: ApplyBattleBgmSetting

The system SHALL the service shall remove the battle bgm field when the battle_bgm setting is disabled.

#### Scenario: BGMDisabled
- **GIVEN** The battle BGM setting is disabled
- **WHEN** the setting is applied
- **THEN** The battle BGM field is omitted

### Requirement: ApplyResultItemColumnsSetting

The system SHALL the service shall remove additem result columns when result_item_columns is disabled.

#### Scenario: ResultItemColumnsDisabled
- **GIVEN** The result item column setting is disabled
- **WHEN** the setting is applied
- **THEN** The addItem columns are omitted

### Requirement: IdentifyMission

The system SHALL the service shall extract the mission identifier named by a request so the corresponding battle bundle can be selected.

#### Scenario: RequestNamesAMission
- **GIVEN** Request plaintext contains a mission identifier
- **WHEN** the mission identifier is resolved
- **THEN** That identifier is returned for battle bundle selection


## Sub-components

> `Battle Response Configuration` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Counter

> Implements: `Counter`

Creates a counter for aggregating repeated values.

#### Requirement: InitializeCounter

The system SHALL the block shall provide a counter that can aggregate occurrences by key.

#### Scenario: NewCounterStartsEmpty
- **GIVEN** No values have been counted
- **WHEN** A counter is created
- **THEN** The counter has no recorded occurrences

### Sub-component: Any

> Implements: `any`

Determines whether at least one item satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAny

The system SHALL the block shall report whether at least one item satisfies the supplied condition.

#### Scenario: MatchingItemExists
- **GIVEN** At least one item satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The block checks the items
- **THEN** It reports true

### Sub-component: SpecGet

> Implements: `spec.get`

Retrieves a value from a specification with an optional fallback.

#### Requirement: ReadSpecValue

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve the requested specification value or its fallback.

#### Scenario: SpecKeyMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The specification does not contain the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The fallback is returned

### Sub-component: CaptureUnitsGet

> Implements: `CAPTURE_UNITS.get`

Retrieves capture-unit configuration for the requested key.

#### Requirement: ReadCaptureUnitSetting

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve the capture-unit setting for a key, using its fallback when absent.

#### Scenario: CaptureSettingMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** No capture-unit setting exists for the key
- **WHEN** The setting is retrieved
- **THEN** The fallback is returned

### Sub-component: SlotGet

> Implements: `slot.get`

Retrieves a value associated with a slot key.

#### Requirement: ReadSlotValue

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve the slot value or its fallback.

#### Scenario: SlotKeyMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The slot has no value for the key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The fallback is returned

### Sub-component: GroupsSetdefault

> Implements: `groups.setdefault`

Ensures a group key has a value and returns that value.

#### Requirement: EnsureGroup

The system SHALL the block shall preserve an existing group value or initialize the key with its default.

#### Scenario: GroupNotPresent
- **GIVEN** The group key is absent
- **WHEN** The block ensures the group
- **THEN** The default is stored and returned

### Sub-component: GroupsGet

> Implements: `groups.get`

Retrieves a group value with an optional fallback.

#### Requirement: ReadGroup

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve a group value or its fallback.

#### Scenario: GroupMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The group key is absent
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The fallback is returned

### Sub-component: PhaseGet

> Implements: `phase.get`

Retrieves a phase value with an optional fallback.

#### Requirement: ReadPhase

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve the phase value or its fallback.

#### Scenario: PhaseKeyMissing
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The phase has no requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The fallback is returned

### Sub-component: OutAppend

> Implements: `out.append`

Adds an item to an output collection.

#### Requirement: AppendOutput

The system SHALL the block shall add the supplied item to the output collection.

#### Scenario: AppendOneItem
- **GIVEN** An output collection and an item are available
- **WHEN** The item is appended
- **THEN** The collection contains the item

### Sub-component: RowGet

> Implements: `row.get`

Retrieves a value from a row with an optional fallback.

The system SHALL the block shall retrieve the requested row value or its fallback.

- **THEN** The fallback is returned

Separates drop-table content into constituent entries.

- **THEN** Each entry is returned separately

> Implements: `src.split`

Separates source content into constituent parts.

- **GIVEN** Source content contains the separator
- **WHEN** The content is split
- **THEN** Its segments are returned

Separates an item into constituent parts.

- **WHEN** The item is split
- **THEN** Its segments are returned

Selects an integer within the requested inclusive bounds.

- **WHEN** The key is removed
- **THEN** Its value is returned and the key is absent

Separates an entry into constituent parts.

- **WHEN** The entry is split
- **THEN** Its segments are returned

- **WHEN** The note block runs
- **THEN** A diagnostic note is recorded

### Sub-component: ReportedDrops

### Sub-component: PerUnitExp

Divides party-total experience evenly among party members and reports the remainder.

#### Scenario: ExperienceNotEvenlyDivisible

- **WHEN** Experience is apportioned
- **THEN** Each member receives the integer quotient and the remainder is returned

- **GIVEN** A story mission ends without a combat victory
- **WHEN** The mission wave count is determined
- **THEN** The original clear is preserved without a battle victory log

> Implements: `mission_id_from`

- **WHEN** The mission identifier is extracted
- **THEN** That identifier is returned for mission-specific bundle selection

- **THEN** The result contains the applicable drops

Retrieves a source value with an optional fallback.

<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The source lacks the requested key
- **WHEN** The value is retrieved
- **THEN** The fallback is returned

### Sub-component: Deepcopy

> Implements: `copy.deepcopy`

Creates an independent deep copy of a value.

#### Requirement: CopyValueDeeply

The system SHALL the block shall produce a copy whose nested mutable values are independent of the source.

#### Scenario: NestedValueCopied

- **WHEN** A deep copy is created
- **THEN** Changing a nested copied value does not change the source

> `source/server/ffbe_battle.py`

### Requirement: BuildMissionLevelInfo

The system SHALL the service shall produce per-party-member level information showing each member’s before and after experience values.

#### Scenario: PartyMembersReceiveExperience
- **GIVEN** A player party and experience gain are available
- **WHEN** mission level information is built
- **THEN** The output includes a before and after experience row for each party member

### Requirement: CalculatePerUnitExperience

The system SHALL the service shall calculate each party member’s share of total mission experience and report the party size and remainder.

#### Scenario: DividePartyExperience
- **GIVEN** A total experience amount is reported
- **WHEN** the per-unit share is calculated
- **THEN** The result contains the per-member share, party size, and remainder

### Requirement: EchoMissionEndResult

The system SHALL the service shall construct the mission-end reply using the values computed and reported by the client.

#### Scenario: ClientReportsMissionResult
- **GIVEN** The request contains a client-computed mission result
- **WHEN** the mission-end reply is built
- **THEN** The reply carries the reported values

### Requirement: ParseMissionResult

The system SHALL the service shall extract mission-end result context for challenge condition evaluation.

#### Scenario: ValidMissionEndReport
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Request plaintext contains mission result data
- **WHEN** the result context is parsed
- **THEN** The extracted context is available to condition evaluators

### Requirement: CreditMissionRewards

The system SHALL the service shall credit the original normal drops once per loaded attempt and restore saved state if crediting fails.

#### Scenario: FirstRewardCreditForAttempt
- **GIVEN** A loaded mission attempt has eligible normal drops
- **WHEN** mission rewards are credited
- **THEN** The original drops are credited once

#### Scenario: RewardCreditFails
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Reward crediting raises an error
- **WHEN** the operation is rolled back
- **THEN** The saved player state is restored


## Sub-components

> `Mission Result Processing` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Counter

> Implements: `Counter`

Counts occurrences of values in mission result data.

#### Requirement: CountValues

The system SHALL the counter shall report the number of occurrences of each value it receives.

#### Scenario: CountsRepeatedValues
- **GIVEN** an iterable containing the same value twice
- **WHEN** a counter is constructed
- **THEN** the value's count is two

### Sub-component: Any

> Implements: `any`

Determines whether any item in an iterable satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAny

The system SHALL the operation shall return true when at least one item satisfies the supplied condition and false otherwise.

#### Scenario: MatchingItemExists
- **GIVEN** an iterable with at least one truthy item
- **WHEN** the iterable is checked
- **THEN** the result is true

### Sub-component: SpecGet

> Implements: `spec.get`

Reads a value from a specification mapping with optional fallback behavior.

#### Requirement: ReadSpecValue

The system SHALL the operation shall return the mapped value when the requested key exists and otherwise use the supplied default.

#### Scenario: MissingKeyUsesDefault
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** a specification without the requested key
- **WHEN** the value is read with a default
- **THEN** the default is returned

### Sub-component: CaptureUnitsGet

> Implements: `CAPTURE_UNITS.get`

Looks up capture-unit data by key.

#### Requirement: LookupCaptureUnits

The system SHALL the operation shall return the capture-unit value for a present key or the supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: UnknownKey
- **GIVEN** a key absent from the capture-unit table
- **WHEN** the key is looked up
- **THEN** the default is returned

### Sub-component: SlotGet

> Implements: `slot.get`

Reads a value from a mission slot mapping.

#### Requirement: ReadSlotValue

The system SHALL the operation shall return the value associated with a slot key or its default.

#### Scenario: SlotHasValue
- **GIVEN** a slot mapping containing the requested key
- **WHEN** the key is read
- **THEN** its associated value is returned

### Sub-component: GroupsSetdefault

> Implements: `groups.setdefault`

Creates or retrieves a grouped result collection for a key.

#### Requirement: EnsureGroup

The system SHALL the operation shall return the existing group for a key or create it using the supplied initial value.

#### Scenario: GroupAbsent
- **GIVEN** a key not present in groups
- **WHEN** the group is ensured
- **THEN** the initial value is stored and returned

### Sub-component: GroupsGet

> Implements: `groups.get`

Reads a grouped result collection by key.

#### Requirement: ReadGroup

The system SHALL the operation shall return the group for a key or the supplied default when no group exists.

#### Scenario: GroupAbsent
- **GIVEN** a key not present in groups
- **WHEN** the group is read
- **THEN** the default is returned

Reads a phase value from phase-related data.

- **THEN** its associated value is returned

### Sub-component: RGet

- **WHEN** experience is divided
- **THEN** the result is per-unit 5, party size 2, remainder 0

> `source/server/ffbe_battle.py`

### Requirement: LoadChallengeTable

The system SHALL the service shall provide challenge identifiers, lapis amounts, condition specifications, and display order grouped by mission.

#### Scenario: LoadShippedChallenges
- **GIVEN** The shipped mission challenge data is available
- **WHEN** the challenge table is requested
- **THEN** Challenges are grouped by mission with their reward, condition, and display order

### Requirement: GetPartySize

The system SHALL the service shall determine the active mission party size from the player’s active party state.

#### Scenario: ActivePartyExists
- **GIVEN** Player state contains an active party
- **WHEN** party size is requested
- **THEN** The number of units in that party is returned

### Requirement: CreditChallengeLapis

The system SHALL the service shall record challenges cleared by the mission result and pay lapis for eligible first clears.

#### Scenario: FirstClearMeetsChallengeConditions
- **GIVEN** The mission result satisfies a challenge not previously cleared
- **WHEN** challenge lapis is credited
- **THEN** The challenge is recorded as cleared and its lapis reward is paid

#### Scenario: ChallengeConditionsAreUnmet
- **GIVEN** The mission result does not satisfy a challenge condition
- **WHEN** challenge lapis is evaluated
- **THEN** That challenge is not credited

### Requirement: ServeChallengeClearState

The system SHALL the service shall attach the mission’s cleared challenges and identify challenges cleared by the current battle.

#### Scenario: ServeMissionChallengeState
- **GIVEN** A mission has previously cleared challenges and may have new clears
- **WHEN** challenge state is served
- **THEN** The response distinguishes prior clears from clears made by this battle

### Requirement: ServeAllChallengeClearState

The system SHALL the service shall attach every mission’s cleared challenge set to a user-state reply.

#### Scenario: ServeAllMissionChallengeState
- **GIVEN** A user-state reply is being prepared
- **WHEN** all challenge clears are served
- **THEN** The response carries cleared challenge sets for every mission


## Sub-components

> `Mission Challenge Rewards` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Counter

> Implements: `Counter`

Aggregates values by key for mission challenge reward processing.

#### Requirement: AggregateCounts

The system SHALL the block must count occurrences by key and expose each key’s accumulated count.

#### Scenario: CountsRepeatedKeys
- **GIVEN** The input contains two occurrences of the same key
- **WHEN** Counts are aggregated
- **THEN** The key's count is two

### Sub-component: Any

> Implements: `any`

Determines whether at least one item satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAny

The system SHALL the block must return true when at least one item satisfies the supplied condition and false otherwise.

#### Scenario: FindsMatchingItem
- **GIVEN** At least one item satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The collection is checked
- **THEN** The result is true

### Sub-component: SpecGet

> Implements: `spec.get`

Reads a condition field from a challenge specification.

#### Requirement: ReadSpecField

The system SHALL the block must return the requested field value or its supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: UsesDefaultForMissingField
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The field is absent from the specification
- **WHEN** The field is read with a default
- **THEN** The default is returned

### Sub-component: CaptureUnitsGet

> Implements: `CAPTURE_UNITS.get`

Looks up the configured capture-unit value for a key.

#### Requirement: LookupCaptureUnits

The system SHALL the block must return the configured capture-unit value or the supplied default for an unknown key.

#### Scenario: ReturnsDefaultForUnknownKey
- **GIVEN** The key is not configured
- **WHEN** Capture units are looked up
- **THEN** The supplied default is returned

### Sub-component: SlotGet

> Implements: `slot.get`

Reads a value from a mission slot record.

#### Requirement: ReadSlotField

The system SHALL the block must return the requested slot value or its supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: ReadsPresentSlotValue
- **GIVEN** The requested field exists in the slot
- **WHEN** The field is read
- **THEN** Its value is returned

### Sub-component: GroupsSetdefault

> Implements: `groups.setdefault`

Creates or retrieves a grouped collection for a mission key.

#### Requirement: EnsureGroup

The system SHALL the block must return the existing group for a key or create and return a group using the supplied default.

#### Scenario: CreatesMissingGroup
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** No group exists for the key
- **WHEN** The group is ensured
- **THEN** A group is created and returned

### Sub-component: GroupsGet

> Implements: `groups.get`

Retrieves a grouped collection by mission key.

#### Requirement: ReadGroup

The system SHALL the block must return the group for a key or the supplied default when no group exists.

#### Scenario: ReturnsDefaultForMissingGroup
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** No group exists for the key
- **WHEN** The group is read
- **THEN** The supplied default is returned

### Sub-component: PhaseGet

> Implements: `phase.get`

Reads the phase value from a mission challenge record.

#### Requirement: ReadPhase

The system SHALL the block must return the phase value or its supplied default when absent.

#### Scenario: ReturnsConfiguredPhase

### Sub-component: TestTownUpdateSwitches

The system SHALL the service shall return the legacy openssl subject hash for a certificate as the first four bytes of the md5 digest of its der-encoded subject, in little-endian hexadecimal form.

<!-- openlore-test: priority=low (auto) -->

### Sub-component: FileExistsError

<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->

- **WHEN** The block attempts to create it
- **THEN** A FileExistsError SHALL indicate the conflict

Write certificate-generation output to its designated file.

- **WHEN** The write operation is performed
- **THEN** The destination file SHALL contain the supplied output

> Implements: `subject_hash_old`

Compute the legacy OpenSSL subject hash from a certificate subject name.

#### Scenario: HashCertificateSubject
- **GIVEN** A certificate with a subject name is supplied
- **WHEN** The legacy subject hash is computed
- **THEN** The result SHALL be the subject DER MD5 digest's first four bytes interpreted as little-endian

> `source/server/ffbe_crypto.py`

### Requirement: ReportBackend

The system SHALL the service shall report which aes backend is active.

- **Implementation**: `backend::source/server/ffbe_crypto.py`

#### Scenario: ReportActiveBackend
- **GIVEN** The crypto service has selected an available backend
- **WHEN** The backend is queried
- **THEN** The service returns either cryptography or openssl

### Requirement: DeriveWireKey

The system SHALL the service shall convert a token into a 16-byte utf-8 key, truncating longer input and padding shorter input with nul bytes.

- **Implementation**: `key16::source/server/ffbe_crypto.py`

#### Scenario: PadShortToken
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A token whose UTF-8 encoding is shorter than 16 bytes
- **WHEN** A wire key is derived
- **THEN** The encoded token is followed by NUL bytes until the key is 16 bytes

#### Scenario: TruncateLongToken
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A token whose UTF-8 encoding exceeds 16 bytes
- **WHEN** A wire key is derived
- **THEN** Only the first 16 encoded bytes are used

### Requirement: DecryptWirePayload

The system SHALL the service shall base64-decode and aes-128-cbc-decrypt a payload, reject ciphertext that is not 16-byte aligned, and validate pkcs5 padding.

- **Implementation**: `cbc_decrypt::source/server/ffbe_crypto.py`

#### Scenario: DecryptValidPayload
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A Base64 ciphertext aligned to 16 bytes and encrypted with valid PKCS5 padding
- **WHEN** The payload is decrypted
- **THEN** The service returns the unpadded plaintext

#### Scenario: RejectUnalignedCiphertext
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A decoded ciphertext whose length is not divisible by 16
- **WHEN** The payload is decrypted
- **THEN** The service raises a ValueError

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidPadding
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A decrypted payload with invalid PKCS5 padding
- **WHEN** The payload is decrypted
- **THEN** The service raises a ValueError

### Requirement: EncryptReply

The system SHALL the service shall aes-128-cbc-encrypt a reply using pkcs5 padding, with optional double padding to match the client request format.

- **Implementation**: `cbc_encrypt::source/server/ffbe_crypto.py`

#### Scenario: EncryptStandardReply
- **GIVEN** Plaintext and a valid AES key
- **WHEN** A reply is encrypted with double_pad set to false
- **THEN** The service returns Base64 ciphertext with standard PKCS5 padding

#### Scenario: EncryptClientCompatibleReply
- **GIVEN** Plaintext and a valid AES key
- **WHEN** A reply is encrypted with double_pad set to true
- **THEN** The service adds the client-compatible extra padding block before encryption

### Requirement: DecryptAesEcbData

The system SHALL the service shall decrypt raw aes-128-ecb data without padding and reject keys or ciphertext that violate the required lengths.

- **Implementation**: `aes_ecb_decrypt::source/server/ffbe_crypto.py`

#### Scenario: DecryptAlignedData
- **GIVEN** A 16-byte key and ciphertext whose length is divisible by 16
- **WHEN** ECB decryption is requested
- **THEN** The service returns decrypted bytes without removing padding

#### Scenario: AcceptEmptyData
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A 16-byte key and empty ciphertext
- **WHEN** ECB decryption is requested
- **THEN** The service returns empty bytes

#### Scenario: RejectInvalidKeyLength
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A key whose length is not 16 bytes
- **WHEN** ECB decryption is requested
- **THEN** The service raises a ValueError

#### Scenario: RejectUnalignedCiphertext
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Ciphertext whose length is not divisible by 16
- **WHEN** ECB decryption is requested
- **THEN** The service raises a ValueError

> `source/server/ffbe_friends.py`

### Requirement: LoadFriends

The system SHALL the service shall load friend entries from the configured yaml file, validate each entry and its unit, and return the roster. it shall reload the roster when the file modification time changes and return an empty roster when the file is missing.

- **Implementation**: `load_friends::source/server/ffbe_friends.py`

#### Scenario: LoadValidRoster
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The YAML file contains valid friend and unit entries
- **WHEN** load_friends is called
- **THEN** The validated roster is returned with unit stats derived from shipped tables

#### Scenario: ReloadAfterFileChange
- **GIVEN** The YAML file has changed since it was last loaded
- **WHEN** load_friends is called
- **THEN** The updated roster is returned

#### Scenario: MissingRosterFile
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The configured YAML file does not exist
- **WHEN** load_friends is called
- **THEN** An empty roster is returned

#### Scenario: InvalidRosterEntry
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The YAML file contains an entry missing required valid data
- **WHEN** load_friends is called
- **THEN** A validation error identifies the malformed entry

### Requirement: ServeFriendReplies

The system SHALL the service shall populate reinforcement and friend-list reply payloads from the local roster for matching reply identifiers, and leave unrelated replies unchanged.

- **Implementation**: `serve_friends::source/server/ffbe_friends.py`

#### Scenario: ServeReinforcementReply
- **GIVEN** The requested reply identifier is the reinforcement reply
- **WHEN** serve_friends is called
- **THEN** The reinforcement reply is populated with roster support units and associated friend and vision-card
data

#### Scenario: ServeFriendListReply
- **GIVEN** The requested reply identifier is the friend-list reply
- **WHEN** serve_friends is called
- **THEN** The friend-list reply is populated from the roster when friend-list serving is enabled

#### Scenario: IgnoreUnrelatedReply
- **GIVEN** The requested reply identifier is neither friend reply
- **WHEN** serve_friends is called
- **THEN** The specification is left unchanged

### Requirement: OpenLocalFriendAction

The system SHALL the service shall handle supported read-only friend-page actions using the local companion roster without modifying player state.

- **Implementation**: `local_friend_action::source/server/ffbe_friends.py`

#### Scenario: OpenLocalFriendPage
- **GIVEN** A supported friend-page action references a roster friend
- **WHEN** local_friend_action is called
- **THEN** The corresponding local friend profile is returned without changing player state

#### Scenario: SearchLocalFriends
- **GIVEN** A supported search action includes a query
- **WHEN** local_friend_action is called
- **THEN** Matching local profiles are returned without changing player state


## Sub-components

> `FriendRosterService` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: LoadFriends

> Implements: `load_friends`

Loads and validates the friend roster from the configured YAML file, refreshing cached data when the file changes.

#### Requirement: LoadRoster

The system SHALL the function shall load friend entries from the configured roster file and return the validated roster.

#### Scenario: LoadExistingRoster
- **GIVEN** The configured file contains valid friend entries
- **WHEN** The roster is loaded
- **THEN** The validated friend entries SHALL be returned

#### Requirement: HandleMissingRoster

The system SHALL the function shall treat a missing roster file as an empty roster without reporting an error.

#### Scenario: MissingFileReturnsEmptyRoster
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The configured roster file does not exist
- **WHEN** The roster is loaded
- **THEN** An empty roster SHALL be returned without an error

#### Requirement: RefreshChangedRoster

The system SHALL the function shall re-read the roster when its modification time changes.

#### Scenario: ChangedFileRefreshesRoster
- **GIVEN** The roster file has changed since it was cached
- **WHEN** The roster is loaded
- **THEN** The returned roster SHALL reflect the changed file contents

### Sub-component: ServeFriends

> Implements: `serve_friends`

Fills the friend and reinforcement reply groups using the loaded friend roster.

#### Requirement: FillFriendReplies

The system SHALL the pipeline step shall populate the two friend replies from the roster.

#### Scenario: FriendReplyIsPopulated
- **GIVEN** The reply specification contains a supported friend reply
- **WHEN** The pipeline step runs
- **THEN** The friend reply SHALL be populated from the roster

#### Requirement: IgnoreOtherReplies

The system SHALL the pipeline step shall leave replies outside its supported friend replies unchanged.

#### Scenario: UnrelatedReplyIsNoOp
- **GIVEN** The reply identifier is not one of the supported friend replies
- **WHEN** The pipeline step runs
- **THEN** The reply specification SHALL remain unchanged

### Sub-component: ReinforcementRow

> Implements: `_reinforce_row`

Builds a roster-backed row for a friend reinforcement reply.

#### Requirement: BuildReinforcementRow

The system SHALL the function shall produce a row representing a friend eligible for reinforcement.

#### Scenario: EligibleFriendHasReinforcementRow
- **GIVEN** A friend entry is eligible for reinforcement
- **WHEN** A reinforcement row is requested
- **THEN** A row representing that friend SHALL be returned

### Sub-component: VcInfoRow

> Implements: `_vc_info_row`

Builds a roster-backed row containing a friend's visitor-center information.

#### Requirement: BuildVisitorCenterInfoRow

The system SHALL the function shall produce a row containing the visitor-center information represented by a friend entry.

#### Scenario: FriendHasVisitorCenterInfo
- **GIVEN** A friend entry contains visitor-center information
- **WHEN** An information row is requested
- **THEN** A row containing that information SHALL be returned

### Sub-component: FriendListRow

> Implements: `_friend_list_row`

Builds a row representing a friend in a friend-list reply.

#### Requirement: BuildFriendListRow

The system SHALL the function shall produce a friend-list row from a friend entry.

#### Scenario: FriendEntryHasListRow
- **GIVEN** A friend entry is available for listing
- **WHEN** A friend-list row is requested
- **THEN** A row representing the friend SHALL be returned

### Sub-component: SpecGet

> Implements: `spec.get`

Retrieves a value from a reply specification by key.

#### Requirement: RetrieveSpecificationValue

The system SHALL the lookup shall return the value associated with the requested key, or the supplied default when the key is absent.

#### Scenario: MissingKeyUsesDefault
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The specification does not contain the requested key
- **WHEN** The key is retrieved with a default value
- **THEN** The default value SHALL be returned

### Sub-component: GroupInsert

> Implements: `group.insert`

Inserts a generated friend-related row into a reply group.

#### Requirement: InsertReplyRow

The system SHALL the insertion shall add the supplied row to the reply group.

#### Scenario: RowAppearsInGroup
- **GIVEN** A reply group and a generated row are available
- **WHEN** The row is inserted
- **THEN** The reply group SHALL contain the row

### Sub-component: TimeStrftime

> Implements: `time.strftime`

Formats the current time for friend-related reply data.

#### Requirement: FormatTimestamp

The system SHALL the formatter shall return a timestamp string matching the requested format.

#### Scenario: TimestampMatchesRequestedFormat
- **GIVEN** A valid time format and time value are provided
- **WHEN** The timestamp is formatted
- **THEN** The returned string SHALL match the requested format

### Sub-component: FavoritesGet

#### Scenario: MissingFavoriteUsesDefault
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->

- **WHEN** The favorite is retrieved with a default value
- **THEN** The default value SHALL be returned

### Sub-component: LegacyFriendRefreshProtection

> Implements: `test_legacy_friend_refresh_cannot_resurrect_deleted_or_pending_helpers`

Verifies that a legacy friend refresh cannot restore deleted or pending helpers.

#### Requirement: PreventLegacyRefreshResurrection

The system SHALL a legacy friend refresh must not resurrect helpers that are deleted or pending.

#### Scenario: DeletedAndPendingHelpersStayAbsent
- **GIVEN** A helper is deleted or pending and a legacy refresh response includes it
- **WHEN** The refresh response is processed
- **THEN** The helper SHALL remain absent from the active friend state

#### Scenario: RestrictServedBanners

#### Scenario: OpenGachaScreen
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->

- **WHEN** Gacha information is served
- **THEN** The authored banner layer is attached to the reply


## Sub-components

> `GachaService` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: CatalogueBanners

- **WHEN** catalogue_banners is called

### Sub-component: NamedBanners

> Implements: `named_banners`

Returns named banners backed by unused real Global banner images when enabled.

#### Requirement: LoadNamedBanners

The system SHALL the named banner collection shall use unused real global banner images when enabled by configuration.

#### Scenario: NamedBannersEnabled
- **GIVEN** GACHA_NAMED is enabled and named banner data is available
- **WHEN** named_banners is called
- **THEN** The named banners are returned

#### Scenario: NamedBannersDisabled
- **GIVEN** GACHA_NAMED=0
- **WHEN** named_banners is called
- **THEN** No named banners are returned

### Sub-component: AuthoredBanners

> Implements: `authored_banners`

Returns the curated authored banners defined in gacha_authored.json.

#### Scenario: ReturnsAuthoredContent
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->

- **WHEN** authored_banners is called

### Sub-component: GachaOnlyIds

> Implements: `gacha_only_ids`

Determines whether the served banner set is restricted to configured GACHA_ONLY IDs.

The system SHALL the id resolver shall return the configured restriction ids from the environment or server/gacha_only.txt, with an empty set meaning unrestricted.

#### Scenario: ConfiguredIds
- **GIVEN** A non-empty GACHA_ONLY value or file contains IDs
- **WHEN** gacha_only_ids is called
- **THEN** The parsed IDs are returned as a set

#### Scenario: NoRestriction
- **GIVEN** No IDs are configured
- **WHEN** gacha_only_ids is called
- **THEN** An empty set is returned
