> `source/server/ffbe_pipeline.py`

### Requirement: ValidatePipeline

The system SHALL the pipeline registry must be checked for consistency between its steps, declared edges, and ordering requirements.

#### Scenario: ConsistentRegistry
- **GIVEN** Every declared dependency is represented by steps in a compatible order
- **WHEN** validate_pipeline is called
- **THEN** An empty list of problems is returned

#### Scenario: ContradictoryRegistry
- **GIVEN** A declared dependency conflicts with the pipeline registry or ordering
- **WHEN** validate_pipeline is called
- **THEN** The registry problems are returned

> `source/server/ffbe_player.py`

### Requirement: ServeLapisShop

The system SHALL the service shall process eligible local lapis bundle purchases using configured prices, validate purchase limits and affordability, and grant the bundle rewards.

- **Implementation**: `serve_lapis_shop::source/server/ffbe_player.py`

#### Scenario: EligiblePurchase
- **GIVEN** A valid bundle is available, within its purchase limit, and the player can afford it
- **WHEN** serve_lapis_shop processes the purchase
- **THEN** The service SHALL spend the required currency, grant the rewards, record the purchase, and update
the reply

### Requirement: ServeDiamonds

The system SHALL the service shall attach the player's lapis balance to replies that refresh user state.

#### Scenario: RefreshBalance
- **GIVEN** A reply refreshes user state
- **WHEN** serve_diamonds processes the reply
- **THEN** The service SHALL include the player's current lapis balance

### Requirement: ApplyPartyEdit

The system SHALL the service shall apply a client's party deck edit to the player's state.

#### Scenario: ValidDeckEdit
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The request contains a party deck edit
- **WHEN** apply_party_edit processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL record the accepted deck changes in player state

### Requirement: ServeUnitFavourite

The system SHALL the service shall apply the requested unit favourite changes and return the resulting lists.

#### Scenario: FavouriteDelta
- **GIVEN** The request contains unit favourite changes
- **WHEN** serve_unit_favourite processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL record the changes and reply with the same resulting lists

### Requirement: ServeUnitStorage

The system SHALL the service shall update storage state for the named roster units.

#### Scenario: UpdateStorage
- **GIVEN** The request names roster units and their storage states
- **WHEN** serve_unit_storage processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL set storage state on the matching roster rows

### Requirement: ServeBeastBoard

The system SHALL the service shall record newly learned or unlocked beast board pieces and acknowledge the request.

#### Scenario: OpenBoardPiece
- **GIVEN** The request identifies a beast board piece to open
- **WHEN** serve_beast_board processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL record the piece as learned or unlocked and acknowledge the request

### Requirement: ServeBeastMix

The system SHALL the service shall apply magicite used for beast mixing and publish newly unlocked trials.

#### Scenario: MixMagicite
- **GIVEN** The request contains beast mixing materials
- **WHEN** serve_beast_mix processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL apply the magicite and include newly unlocked trials in the reply

### Requirement: ServeUnitSell

The system SHALL the service shall remove sold roster units and credit the client's gil amount.

#### Scenario: SellRosterUnits
- **GIVEN** The request identifies units to sell and the client's gil credit
- **WHEN** serve_unit_sell processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL remove the sold units and credit the specified gil

### Requirement: ServeUnitExchange

The system SHALL the service shall convert native enhancement materials into their corresponding point balance.

- **Implementation**: `serve_unit_exchange::source/server/ffbe_player.py`

#### Scenario: ExchangeMaterials
- **GIVEN** The request contains eligible enhancement materials
- **WHEN** serve_unit_exchange processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL convert the materials and update the corresponding point balance

### Requirement: ServeUnitMix

The system SHALL the service shall fuse eligible materials into a base unit and return the mix result.

#### Scenario: FuseMaterials
- **GIVEN** The request identifies a base unit and eligible materials
- **WHEN** serve_unit_mix processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL apply the fusion and return the UnitMixResponse

### Requirement: ServeFavouriteInfo

The system SHALL the service shall attach the player's cumulative unit favourite list to user-state replies.

#### Scenario: AttachFavourites
- **GIVEN** A user-state reply is being prepared
- **WHEN** serve_favourite_info processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL attach the cumulative favourite list

### Requirement: EchoClientGroup

The system SHALL the service shall echo the client's own group value in the reply.

#### Scenario: EchoGroup
- **GIVEN** The request contains a client group value
- **WHEN** echo_client_group processes it
- **THEN** The reply SHALL contain that client-provided group

### Requirement: ServeSwitches

The system SHALL the service shall rewrite tracked switch containers in a reply from the player's tracked switch state.

#### Scenario: RewriteTrackedSwitches
- **GIVEN** The reply contains tracked switch types
- **WHEN** serve_switches processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL populate each tracked switch container from the tracked state

### Requirement: ServeHomeBadges

The system SHALL the service shall replace event badge information in a reply with the current season's badge set.

#### Scenario: SeasonBadges
- **GIVEN** A reply carries EventBadgeInfo
- **WHEN** serve_home_badges processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL set the badge value to the season's badge set

### Requirement: ServeLoginBonus

The system SHALL the service shall answer login bonus requests with the selected month's 28 days based on the player's counter.

#### Scenario: MonthlyCalendar
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A login bonus request selects a month
- **WHEN** serve_login_bonus processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL return that month's 28 days using the player's login counter

### Requirement: ServeMail

The system SHALL the service shall serve the mailbox from server-authoritative player state.

#### Scenario: ReadMailbox
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A mailbox request is received
- **WHEN** serve_mail processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL return the server-authoritative mailbox state

### Requirement: DeliverLoginMail

The system SHALL the service shall place the daily login award in the player's inbox.

#### Scenario: DeliverDailyAward
- **GIVEN** A daily login award is due
- **WHEN** deliver_login_mail processes it
- **THEN** The service SHALL add the award to the inbox

### Requirement: DeliverAnniversaryTicket

The system SHALL the service shall deliver one eighth-anniversary ticket per calendar day as a normal mailbox entry.

- **Implementation**: `deliver_anniversary_ticket::source/server/ffbe_player.py`

#### Scenario: DailyTicketDelivery
- **GIVEN** No eighth-anniversary ticket has been delivered today
- **WHEN** deliver_anniversary_ticket processes the request
- **THEN** The service SHALL add one ordinary mailbox entry for the ticket

### Requirement: ScoreQuestAction

The system SHALL the service shall score verified player activity against the original quest definitions, optionally recording best progress.

- **Implementation**: `score_quest_action::source/server/ffbe_player.py`

#### Scenario: VerifiedActivity
- **GIVEN** An activity matches an original quest definition
- **WHEN** score_quest_action processes the activity
- **THEN** The service SHALL update the matching quest progress by the specified amount

> `source/server/ffbe_state.py`

### Requirement: LoadEndpoints

The system SHALL the server must load endpoint definitions and request mappings from the configured path.

#### Scenario: LoadEndpointConfiguration
- **GIVEN** A configured endpoint file is available
- **WHEN** load_endpoints is called
- **THEN** It returns endpoint definitions and request mappings parsed from that file

### Requirement: SelectEndpoint

The system SHALL the server must select the request class using the exact request id when supplied, accounting for urls shared by multiple request classes.

#### Scenario: SelectByExactRequestID
- **GIVEN** A request ID maps to an endpoint
- **WHEN** endpoint_for is called with that ID
- **THEN** The matching endpoint is selected even if its URL is shared

### Requirement: ReloadRepliesWhenChanged

The system SHALL the server must reload configured reply bodies when the replies file changes on disk.

#### Scenario: RepliesFileChanges
- **GIVEN** The replies file has changed since the last load
- **WHEN** reload_replies_if_changed is called
- **THEN** Reply bodies are re-read from the file

### Requirement: WarnAboutNewerSource

The system SHALL the server should report when a server source file is newer than the running process start time.

#### Scenario: SourceChangedAfterProcessStart
- **GIVEN** A server source file has a modification time later than process start
- **WHEN** warn_if_source_is_newer is called
- **THEN** A diagnostic warning is printed

### Requirement: ReloadSeasonWhenChanged

The system SHALL the server must reload season configuration when the selected season or season file changes.

#### Scenario: SeasonConfigurationChanges
- **GIVEN** The season selector or season file differs from the last loaded version
- **WHEN** reload_season_if_changed is called
- **THEN** Season configuration and its associated campaign data are refreshed

### Requirement: RepairLegacyResourceSkips

The system SHALL the server must remove only legacy resource skip entries known to have been injected by the former android loader.

- **Implementation**: `repair_legacy_resource_skips::source/server/ffbe_state.py`

#### Scenario: RepairKnownLegacySkips
<!-- openlore-test: priority=low (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player has the exact legacy skip bitmap and the resource is available
- **WHEN** repair_legacy_resource_skips is called
- **THEN** The injected skip entries are removed without changing unrelated entries

### Requirement: LoadPlayerState

The system SHALL the server must build the active player state from the configured state file, migrating supported older state and using a fresh state if the file is invalid.

#### Scenario: LoadPersistedPlayerState
- **GIVEN** A valid state file exists
- **WHEN** load_state is called
- **THEN** The player state is loaded and applicable configured sets and grants are applied

#### Scenario: RecoverFromInvalidState
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The state file is invalid or unreadable
- **WHEN** load_state is called
- **THEN** A fresh player state is initialized

### Requirement: SavePlayerState

The system SHALL the server must persist the active player state when it has changed.

#### Scenario: SaveChangedState
- **GIVEN** The active player state has changes to persist
- **WHEN** save_state is called
- **THEN** The state is saved through PlayerState persistence


## Sub-components

> `ServerState` is an orchestrator. Each sub-component below implements one logical block.

### Sub-component: Path

> Implements: `Path`

Provides filesystem path handling for server configuration and persisted state.

#### Requirement: CreatePath

The system SHALL the block shall represent the supplied path for filesystem operations.

#### Scenario: PathIsRepresented
- **GIVEN** A filesystem path is supplied
- **WHEN** Path is called
- **THEN** A path object representing that path is returned

### Sub-component: SwitchPosition

> Implements: `switch_position`

Determines whether a switch position is available for the player's state.

#### Requirement: ResolveSwitchPosition

The system SHALL the block shall determine the availability of a requested switch position.

#### Scenario: PositionAvailabilityIsDetermined
- **GIVEN** Season data and a requested switch position are available
- **WHEN** The switch position is checked
- **THEN** The block returns whether that position is available

### Sub-component: All

> Implements: `all`

Checks whether every item in a collection satisfies a condition.

#### Requirement: CheckAllItems

The system SHALL the block shall report whether all items in the supplied collection satisfy the condition.

#### Scenario: AllItemsSatisfyCondition
- **GIVEN** Every item in a collection satisfies the condition
- **WHEN** The collection is checked
- **THEN** The result is true

### Sub-component: MapExtUnservableIgnores

> Implements: `map_ext_unservable_ignores`

Identifies unavailable map extensions to ignore while constructing map data.

#### Requirement: IdentifyUnservableExtensions

The system SHALL the block shall identify map extensions that are not served and exclude them from the returned map data.

#### Scenario: UnservableExtensionIsExcluded
- **GIVEN** A map row refers to an extension that is not served
- **WHEN** Map extension ignores are calculated
- **THEN** That extension is excluded from the returned map data

### Sub-component: LedgerGet

> Implements: `ledger.get`

Retrieves a value from the player's resource ledger.

#### Requirement: RetrieveLedgerValue

#### Scenario: ExistingLedgerValueIsReturned
- **GIVEN** The ledger contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The key is retrieved
- **THEN** The corresponding ledger value is returned

### Sub-component: JsonLoads

> Implements: `json.loads`

Parses serialized JSON data into a structured value.

#### Requirement: ParseJson

The system SHALL the block shall parse valid json text into its corresponding structured value.

#### Scenario: ValidJsonIsParsed
- **GIVEN** A valid JSON document is supplied
- **WHEN** The document is parsed
- **THEN** The corresponding structured value is returned

### Sub-component: PathReadText

#### Scenario: ExistingFileTextIsRead

- **WHEN** Its text is read
- **THEN** The file content is returned as text

- **WHEN** Its values are requested
- **THEN** The stored position values are provided

> Implements: `signature.setdefault`

<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->

### Sub-component: PositionsItems

> Implements: `positions.items`

> Implements: `test_legacy_scene_skip_repair_is_narrow_and_runs_once`

Verifies that legacy scene skip repair is narrow in scope and runs only once.

#### Scenario: RepairIsNarrowAndSingleRun

Undo only the exact resource skip bitmap injected by the former Android loader.

> `source/server/gacha_archive.py`

### Requirement: IdentifyGlobalEnglishBannerArt

The system SHALL the archive shall identify whether a banner uses global english art based on the saved global cache or community calendar.

- **Implementation**: `english_art::source/server/gacha_archive.py`

#### Scenario: GlobalEnglishArtIsAvailable
- **GIVEN** A banner has art recorded in the saved Global cache or community calendar
- **WHEN** The archive checks its art
- **THEN** It reports that the banner uses Global English art

### Requirement: DetermineOriginalBannerTab

The system SHALL the archive shall determine the original tab for a banner from its gachamst type code and banner attributes.

- **Implementation**: `wire_type::source/server/gacha_archive.py`

#### Scenario: BannerHasARecognizedType
- **GIVEN** A banner and its GachaMst type code are available
- **WHEN** The archive determines its original tab
- **THEN** It returns the tab corresponding to that banner

### Requirement: ClassifyPermanentSummons

The system SHALL the archive shall identify always-available summons, optionally using standard summon families for classification.

- **Implementation**: `permanent::source/server/gacha_archive.py`

#### Scenario: AlwaysAvailableSummon
- **GIVEN** A banner matches a permanent summon name or family
- **WHEN** The archive classifies the banner
- **THEN** It marks the banner as permanent

### Requirement: BuildBannerCatalogue

The system SHALL the archive shall group non-permanent banners into exactly one browsing group and include permanent summons with each group.

#### Scenario: CatalogueContainsBanners
- **GIVEN** A collection of banners includes permanent and non-permanent summons
- **WHEN** The archive builds the catalogue
- **THEN** Each non-permanent banner appears in exactly one group and permanent summons are available in every
group

### Requirement: SelectBrowsingGroup

The system SHALL the archive shall determine the selected browsing group for a player from the available groups.

- **Implementation**: `selected_group::source/server/gacha_archive.py`

#### Scenario: PlayerHasAGroupSelection
- **GIVEN** A player and available groups are provided
- **WHEN** The archive resolves the player's selection
- **THEN** It returns the applicable selected group

### Requirement: FilterVisibleBanners

The system SHALL the archive shall determine which banners are visible to a player from the banner collection and player state.

- **Implementation**: `visible_banners::source/server/gacha_archive.py`

#### Scenario: PlayerViewsAvailableBanners
- **GIVEN** A player and banner collection are provided
- **WHEN** The archive filters banners for display
- **THEN** It returns the banners visible to that player

### Requirement: BuildArchiveMenu

The system SHALL the archive shall produce the browsing menu for a player, optionally honoring an explicit selection.

#### Scenario: MenuRequestedWithASelection
- **GIVEN** A player, banners, and an optional group selection are provided
- **WHEN** The archive builds the menu
- **THEN** It returns the menu for the applicable group

> `source/server/gacha_authored.json`

### Requirement: LoadAuthoredBanners

The system SHALL the service shall load the authored banner definitions and reject entries that violate the supported schema or reference invalid shipped master data.

#### Scenario: ValidAuthoredContent
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The authored file contains supported banner definitions whose referenced IDs exist in shipped
masters
- **WHEN** The content is loaded
- **THEN** Validated banner definitions are made available to the gacha server

#### Scenario: InvalidReferencedID
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A banner references a unit or ticket group absent from its shipped master
- **WHEN** The content is loaded
- **THEN** The invalid reference is reported as an error

### Requirement: BuildGachaBannerResponses

The system SHALL the service shall convert validated banner definitions into gacha master response rows, using shipped localized identity and derived unit pools.

#### Scenario: TierBasedPool
- **GIVEN** A banner defines rarity tiers without explicit unit lists
- **WHEN** The response rows are built
- **THEN** Each tier is expanded from shipped base-rarity unit roots and per-unit rates are derived

#### Scenario: LocalizedBannerIdentity
- **GIVEN** A banner ID has shipped localized title and description text
- **WHEN** The response rows are built
- **THEN** The displayed identity comes from the shipped text keyed by that ID

### Requirement: ProcessGachaDraw

The system SHALL the service shall execute a banner draw using its configured cost, draw count, pool rates, guarantee, ticket group, and step progression.

#### Scenario: GuaranteedDraw
- **GIVEN** A draw request uses a banner with a configured rarity or unit guarantee
- **WHEN** The draw is processed
- **THEN** At least one result satisfies the configured guarantee

#### Scenario: StepUpProgression
- **GIVEN** A player executes a step-up banner step
- **WHEN** The draw is processed
- **THEN** The configured step cost and draw count are applied and step state advances

> `source/server/gacha_box.py`

### Requirement: GetBoxState

The system SHALL the service shall return the current gacha box state for a player and banner.

- **Implementation**: `state::source/server/gacha_box.py`

#### Scenario: StateExists
- **GIVEN** A player has saved state for the banner
- **WHEN** The state is requested
- **THEN** The saved state is returned without altering the stored state

#### Scenario: StateIsNew
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The player has no saved state for the banner
- **WHEN** The state is requested
- **THEN** A valid initial state is returned

### Requirement: GetDetailId

The system SHALL the service shall identify the active detail record for a player's banner state.

- **Implementation**: `detail_id::source/server/gacha_box.py`

#### Scenario: ActiveDetailAvailable
- **GIVEN** The player's banner state identifies an active detail
- **WHEN** The detail identifier is requested
- **THEN** The active detail identifier is returned

### Requirement: GetBoxId

The system SHALL the service shall identify the current box for a player's banner state.

- **Implementation**: `box_id::source/server/gacha_box.py`

#### Scenario: CurrentBoxAvailable
- **GIVEN** The player's banner state identifies a current box
- **WHEN** The box identifier is requested
- **THEN** The current box identifier is returned

### Requirement: GetGachaGroups

The system SHALL the service shall build the gacha presentation groups for a player and banner, including applicable details, buttons, and schedule information.

- **Implementation**: `groups::source/server/gacha_box.py`

#### Scenario: GroupsForAvailableBanner
- **GIVEN** A banner has configured gacha details and presentation data
- **WHEN** Groups are requested
- **THEN** Groups are returned with the applicable detail and draw options

### Requirement: RollGacha

The system SHALL the service shall validate a requested draw, apply its cost and draw rules, award eligible units or items, and update player and banner counters.

- **Implementation**: `roll::source/server/gacha_box.py`

#### Scenario: ValidDraw
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested detail is available and the player can pay its cost
- **WHEN** A draw is processed
- **THEN** The cost is spent, rewards are selected and granted, and relevant counters are updated

#### Scenario: InvalidRequestedDetail
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke,regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The requested detail does not match the player's active banner detail
- **WHEN** A draw is processed
- **THEN** The draw is rejected with a validation error

#### Scenario: InsufficientFunds
- **GIVEN** The player cannot meet the requested draw cost
- **WHEN** A draw is processed
- **THEN** The draw is not completed and no rewards are granted

### Requirement: AdvanceBox

The system SHALL the service shall process a box-advance request for the selected banner and return the resulting box and draw information.

- **Implementation**: `next_box::source/server/gacha_box.py`

#### Scenario: AdvanceSelectedBannerBox
- **GIVEN** The request identifies a banner with an available box draw
- **WHEN** The box is advanced
- **THEN** The applicable box state and resulting draw information are returned

### Sub-component: RollGacha

> Implements: `roll_gacha`

Processes a gacha roll by determining eligibility and costs, selecting rewards, applying bonuses and guarantees, and updating player and banner state.

#### Requirement: ValidateAndResolveRoll

The system SHALL the system shall determine whether the requested roll is available for the current period and banner state and resolve its applicable pool and cost.

#### Scenario: EligibleRollIsResolved
- **GIVEN** the banner is active and the player satisfies roll requirements
- **WHEN** the roll is requested
- **THEN** the applicable pool and cost are resolved

#### Scenario: IneligibleRollIsRejected
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** the banner is inactive or the player fails a roll requirement
- **WHEN** the roll is requested
- **THEN** the roll does not proceed

#### Requirement: ChargeRollCost

The system SHALL the system shall charge the applicable currency or items for a roll before awarding its rewards.

#### Scenario: AffordableRollIsCharged
- **GIVEN** the player has sufficient required currency or items
- **WHEN** the roll is processed
- **THEN** the required cost is deducted

#### Requirement: SelectAndAwardRewards

The system SHALL the system shall select rewards according to the resolved roll rules and grant the resulting units, cards, or items to the player.

#### Scenario: SelectedRewardsAreGranted
- **GIVEN** the roll has a valid reward pool
- **WHEN** reward selection completes
- **THEN** each selected reward is granted and represented in the roll result

#### Requirement: ApplyBonusesAndGuarantees

The system SHALL the system shall apply configured step, milestone, lap, and guarantee rewards that qualify for the roll.

#### Scenario: QualifyingMilestoneBonusIsAdded
- **GIVEN** the roll reaches a configured milestone
- **WHEN** the roll rewards are finalized
- **THEN** the configured milestone reward is included

#### Requirement: UpdateRollState

The system SHALL the system shall update and return the banner state and roll result after processing the roll.

#### Scenario: SuccessfulRollAdvancesState
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** a roll completes successfully
- **WHEN** the outcome is finalized
- **THEN** the returned state reflects the roll and its rewards

> `source/server/gacha_campaigns.json`

### Requirement: ServeGachaBanners

The system SHALL the server shall serve the evergreen standard banners together with all campaign banners when no campaign is selected, or with the banners from the selected campaign when a campaign key is provided.

#### Scenario: ServeAllCampaignsByDefault
- **GIVEN** No GACHA_CAMPAIGN value is set
- **WHEN** the capture server selects gacha banners
- **THEN** it serves the evergreen standard banners and banners from all campaigns

#### Scenario: ServeOneCampaign
- **GIVEN** GACHA_CAMPAIGN is set to a valid campaign key
- **WHEN** the capture server selects gacha banners
- **THEN** it serves the evergreen standard banners and banners from that campaign

#### Scenario: CampaignSelectionDoesNotImposeADateGate
- **GIVEN** a campaign has a configured date window
- **WHEN** that campaign is selected
- **THEN** its banners are selected by campaign key rather than filtered by the current date

### Requirement: ClassifyGachaBanners

The system SHALL the server shall assign each served banner a tab classification based on the recovered classifier for its gacha type, using the server's type-code encoding.

#### Scenario: ClassifyAStandardBanner
- **GIVEN** a banner has a gacha type mapped to a known tab
- **WHEN** the server classifies the banner
- **THEN** the banner is assigned the corresponding tab

> `source/server/gacha_rates.py`

### Requirement: BuildBannerRatesPage

The system SHALL the service shall assemble a rates page for the requested banner, including its available offers, costs, guarantees, and reward rate tables.

- **Implementation**: `page::source/server/gacha_rates.py`

#### Scenario: KnownBanner
- **GIVEN** A banner exists with offers and reward pools
- **WHEN** A rates page is requested for that banner
- **THEN** The page includes the banner’s offers and formatted reward rate tables

#### Scenario: BannerNotFound
- **GIVEN** No banner matches the requested identifier
- **WHEN** A rates page is requested
- **THEN** The service returns its not-found response

### Requirement: FormatRewardName

The system SHALL the service shall produce a display name for a reward using the available reward and item naming data.

- **Implementation**: `reward_name::source/server/gacha_rates.py`

#### Scenario: RecognizedReward
- **GIVEN** A reward has a recognized item or unit identifier
- **WHEN** Its display name is requested
- **THEN** The service returns the corresponding readable name

#### Scenario: UnrecognizedReward
- **GIVEN** A reward has no matching name data
- **WHEN** Its display name is requested
- **THEN** The service returns a fallback name derived from the reward data

### Requirement: FormatRewardRateTable

The system SHALL the service shall format a reward pool as a table of reward entries and rates, accounting for mixed pools when requested.

#### Scenario: StandardPool
- **GIVEN** A reward pool with rate data
- **WHEN** The pool is formatted without mixed mode
- **THEN** The service returns a table of its reward entries and rates

#### Scenario: MixedPool
- **GIVEN** A mixed reward pool with rate data
- **WHEN** The pool is formatted in mixed mode
- **THEN** The service returns a table reflecting the mixed pool’s rates

### Requirement: LoadItemNames

The system SHALL the service shall provide item naming data used to display rewards.

#### Scenario: ItemNamesAvailable
- **GIVEN** Item naming data is available
- **WHEN** Item names are requested
- **THEN** The service returns the item name mapping

### Requirement: GenerateStandardBanners

The system SHALL the generator shall emit a banner for each id in the configured standard summon families whose shipped name matches that family, using authored schedule, cost, reward pool, detail id, and art mapping.

#### Scenario: EmitOnlyNamedFamilyIDs
- **GIVEN** A configured family ID block contains IDs with and without shipped names matching the family
- **WHEN** Standard banners are generated
- **THEN** A banner is emitted for each matching named ID and title-less or non-matching IDs are omitted

#### Scenario: PopulateAuthoredBannerDetails
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A matching family ID has authored schedule, cost, reward pool, and art mapping
- **WHEN** Its banner is generated
- **THEN** The banner includes those authored details and is marked as standard-authored

### Requirement: BuildArenaRewardPool

The system SHALL the generator shall build the arena summon reward pool from the configured local enhancer and consumable replacements, with rates split equally within each group.

#### Scenario: SplitArenaPoolRatesByGroup
- **GIVEN** The local Arena enhancer and consumable reward groups are configured
- **WHEN** The Arena reward pool is built
- **THEN** Enhancers receive 75% of the total rate and consumables receive 25%, split equally within each group

#### Scenario: ExcludeRareSummonHeroes
- **GIVEN** The local Arena reward pool is built
- **WHEN** Its entries are emitted
- **THEN** No rare-summon heroes appear in the pool

### Requirement: InitializeSeason

The system SHALL the service shall initialize a season from its key and configuration specification.

#### Scenario: InitializeConfiguredSeason
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** A season key and configuration specification
- **WHEN** The season is initialized
- **THEN** The instance exposes the season configuration through its accessors

### Requirement: CheckCatchupSchedules

The system SHALL the service shall indicate whether the season enables any catch-up schedule.

#### Scenario: CatchUpEnabled
- **GIVEN** The season configures a ladder, campaign, or welcome catch-up schedule
- **WHEN** Catch-up status is requested
- **THEN** The service returns true

#### Scenario: CatchUpDisabled
- **GIVEN** The season configures none of those schedules
- **WHEN** Catch-up status is requested
- **THEN** The service returns false

### Requirement: GetSpecialLoginBonusCampaign

The system SHALL the service shall return the special login bonus reward group configured for the season, or an empty string when none is configured.

#### Scenario: CampaignConfigured
- **GIVEN** The season specifies a special login bonus campaign group
- **WHEN** The campaign group is requested
- **THEN** The configured group id is returned

#### Scenario: NoCampaignConfigured
- **GIVEN** The season specifies no special login bonus campaign
- **WHEN** The campaign group is requested
- **THEN** An empty string is returned

### Requirement: GetWelcomeGrant

The system SHALL the service shall return the authored fresh-account currency grant for the season.

#### Scenario: WelcomeGrantConfigured
- **GIVEN** The season defines fresh-account currency amounts
- **WHEN** The welcome grant is requested
- **THEN** The configured currency amounts are returned

#### Scenario: WelcomeGrantAbsent
- **GIVEN** The season is off or defines no welcome grant
- **WHEN** The welcome grant is requested
- **THEN** An empty mapping is returned

### Requirement: GetPanelQuestGroups

The system SHALL the service shall return the panel quest group ids enabled for the season.

#### Scenario: PanelQuestGroupsConfigured
- **GIVEN** The season specifies panel quest groups
- **WHEN** Panel quest groups are requested
- **THEN** The configured group ids are returned

#### Scenario: PanelQuestGroupsAbsent
- **GIVEN** The season specifies no panel quest groups
- **WHEN** Panel quest groups are requested
- **THEN** An empty list is returned and the panel quest screen stays shut

### Requirement: GetSpecialChallengeGroups

The system SHALL the service shall return the special challenge group ids enabled for the season.

#### Scenario: ChallengeGroupsConfigured
- **GIVEN** The season specifies special challenge groups
- **WHEN** Challenge groups are requested
- **THEN** The configured group ids are returned

#### Scenario: ChallengeGroupsAbsent
- **GIVEN** The season specifies no special challenge groups
- **WHEN** Challenge groups are requested
- **THEN** An empty list is returned so the default applies

### Requirement: GetDailyQuestSelection

The system SHALL the service shall return the daily quest pool selection and requested quest count.

#### Scenario: DailyQuestSelectionConfigured
- **GIVEN** The season defines daily quest pools and a count
- **WHEN** The daily quest selection is requested
- **THEN** The configured pools and count are returned

### Requirement: GetBadgeString

The system SHALL the service shall return the season badge configuration as a comma-joined string.

#### Scenario: BadgesConfigured
- **GIVEN** The season defines badge entries
- **WHEN** The badge string is requested
- **THEN** The entries are returned joined by commas

### Requirement: GetSwitchSets

The system SHALL the service shall return the feature switch bits forced on by the season.

#### Scenario: ValidSwitchBitsConfigured
<!-- openlore-test: tags=smoke (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The season defines recognized switch bits
- **WHEN** Switch sets are requested
- **THEN** The configured bits are returned grouped by type

#### Scenario: InvalidSwitchBitConfigured
<!-- openlore-test: tags=regression (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** The season includes an unrecognized switch bit
- **WHEN** Switch sets are requested
- **THEN** The invalid bit is skipped without guessing its meaning

### Requirement: GetUnknownBadgeKeys

The system SHALL the service shall report badge keys that are not recognized.

#### Scenario: UnknownBadgeKeysPresent
- **GIVEN** The season contains unrecognized badge keys
- **WHEN** Unknown badge keys are requested
- **THEN** Those keys are returned

### Requirement: SummarizeSeason

The system SHALL the service shall provide a summary of the season configuration.

#### Scenario: SeasonSummaryRequested
- **GIVEN** A season instance
- **WHEN** Its summary is requested
- **THEN** A textual summary of the season is returned

### Requirement: ListSeasons

The system SHALL the service shall list available season keys from the season configuration file.

- **Implementation**: `list_keys::source/server/season.py`

#### Scenario: SeasonFileContainsKeys
- **GIVEN** A readable season configuration file
- **WHEN** Season keys are listed
- **THEN** The available keys are returned

### Requirement: LoadSeason

The system SHALL the service shall resolve and load the active season, preferring an explicit name, then the season environment value, then the file default; an unknown name shall fall back to the off configuration.

#### Scenario: ExplicitSeasonSelected
- **GIVEN** An explicit season name is supplied
- **WHEN** The season is loaded
- **THEN** The matching season configuration is used

#### Scenario: EnvironmentSeasonSelected
- **GIVEN** No explicit name is supplied and SEASON names a configured season
- **WHEN** The season is loaded
- **THEN** The environment-selected season configuration is used

#### Scenario: DefaultSeasonSelected
- **GIVEN** No explicit name or usable environment selection exists
- **WHEN** The season is loaded
- **THEN** The file's default season configuration is used

#### Scenario: UnknownSeasonSelected
- **GIVEN** The resolved season name is unknown
- **WHEN** The season is loaded
- **THEN** The off configuration is used

The system SHALL the operation shall produce merged login bonus configuration from the supplied sources.

#### Scenario: CombinesLoginBonusSources
<!-- openlore-test: priority=high (auto) -->
- **GIVEN** Multiple login bonus configuration sources are supplied
- **WHEN** The login bonus configurations are merged
- **THEN** The result contains the applicable login bonus entries

### Sub-component: MergeEvents

> Implements: `_merge_events`

Combines event configuration from the relevant season sources.

#### Requirement: MergeEventConfiguration

The system SHALL the operation shall produce merged event configuration from the supplied sources.

#### Scenario: CombinesEventSources
- **GIVEN** Multiple event configuration sources are supplied
- **WHEN** The event configurations are merged
- **THEN** The result contains the applicable event entries

### Sub-component: Season

> Implements: `Season`

Loads and exposes season configuration used to govern server behavior.

#### Requirement: InitializeSeasonConfiguration

The system SHALL the operation shall make the season configuration available, including applicable schedules, rewards, quests, badges, and feature switches.

#### Scenario: ExposesLoadedSeasonSettings
- **GIVEN** Valid season configuration is available
- **WHEN** A Season instance is initialized
- **THEN** Its configuration exposes the applicable season settings

### Sub-component: DocumentGet

> Implements: `doc.get`

Retrieves a configuration value from a document by key.

#### Requirement: RetrieveDocumentValue

The system SHALL the operation shall return the value associated with the requested key when present.

#### Scenario: ReturnsPresentValue
- **GIVEN** The document contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The key is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned

### Sub-component: LowerKey

> Implements: `key.lower`

Converts a key to lowercase for normalized configuration lookup.

#### Requirement: NormalizeKeyCase

The system SHALL the operation shall return the key converted to lowercase.

#### Scenario: ConvertsUppercaseCharacters
- **GIVEN** The key contains uppercase characters
- **WHEN** The key is lowercased
- **THEN** The returned key contains lowercase characters

### Sub-component: GetSeasonEntry

> Implements: `seasons.get`

Retrieves a season entry by key from the seasons collection.

#### Requirement: RetrieveSeasonEntry

The system SHALL the operation shall return the season entry associated with the requested key when present.

#### Scenario: ReturnsMatchingSeason
- **GIVEN** The seasons collection contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The season entry is retrieved
- **THEN** The matching season entry is returned

### Sub-component: GetSpecificationValue

> Implements: `spec.get`

Retrieves a configuration value from a specification by key.

#### Requirement: RetrieveSpecificationValue

The system SHALL the operation shall return the value associated with the requested key when present.

#### Scenario: ReturnsPresentSpecificationValue
- **GIVEN** The specification contains the requested key
- **WHEN** The key is retrieved
- **THEN** The associated value is returned
